V466: **awaiting a promise w/ ⊥ failure mode is a HANG, ⊥ a wait.** `browserMediaEnvironment.openFile` awaited `video.play()`; on a source that never decodes it NEVER settles ∴ the open loop stranded BEFORE `registerMediaSource` & the diagnostic written to name it never ran — a hang sitting between 2 CORRECT halves, ∀ unit suite green. ∴ kick such a call, ⊥ await it; gate readiness on an EVENT (`loadedmetadata`) w/ an error path & a TIMEOUT (T493,V369,V363)
V467: a duplicate-registration guard ! be scoped to the THING REGISTERED INTO (the bus), ⊥ to the component instance. `useRef(new Set<bus>())` guards one mount only ∴ a 2nd mount on the same bus throws. 3 sites found (webmcp B108, `useRuntimeCommands`, T292's miss) (T493,T531)
V468: **before committing a file you did ⊥ write, RESOLVE ITS IMPORTS against the tree you commit INTO.** `git diff --cached` shows foreign hunks; it does ⊥ show that a foreign hunk IMPORTS something you ⊥ carry. T508 swept T493's uncommitted `loop-continuity.test.ts` hunk ∴ the import reached HEAD w/o its module & V449's gate was RED for **15 commits** — a test split from its module by a party who wrote NEITHER. mechanical check, catchable @ the sweep|B109,T493,T508
V469: an UNHANDLED ERROR ≠ a FAILED TEST. `URL.revokeObjectURL is not a function` (`project-io.ts:118`, fires ∈ a timeout) leaves `audio-track-capture.test.tsx` at **6 passed / 1 error** ∴ it VANISHES from a summary counting FAILURES & REAPPEARS ∈ one counting ERRORS. 2 sessions read it as "fixed". ∴ report BOTH counts, ∨ a suite can be called green while throwing|B110
V470: **READ the output space from the PLAN, ⊥ assume it.** the Output node APPLIES the display transform ∴ its target already holds ENCODED bytes; both look harnesses assumed `linear` & encoded a 2ND time ∴ ∀ look-pass PNG read ~1.5 stops TOO PALE. proven analytically ⊥ by eye: a bare perlin4d field measures median **0.738** assumed-linear vs **0.503** at the plan's actual space — & 0.503 is exactly `noise.wgsl`'s `0.5+0.5·shaped·amp` for a zero-mean field, while 0.738 = srgb_encode(0.5). a wrong LOOK harness makes every look judgement wrong QUIETLY (T518,T513,V461)
V471: **WHAT "BEAUTIFUL & SHOWCASING" MEANS — measured from the owner's own file (T538), ⊥ from taste.** 8 ideas, ∀ citable: (1) **1 source, 3 READINGS** — 3 `renderPoints` over the SAME cloud, split by GROUP PREDICATE, each w/ own colour+size (deep blue ungrouped; orange `p.velocity.y>0.04`; cyan `p.velocity.y>0.17`). structure from SELECTION, ⊥ from more nodes. (2) **the kernel WRITES data for downstream selection** — `q.velocity = vec3f(field, creases, drive)` ∴ velocity = an ATTRIBUTE CARRIER & the groups slice on HOW CREASED a point is. (3) **gain+bias PER BAND** — 8 `valueMath` multiply→add PAIRS, each mapping ONE band (low/lowMid/high/highMid/level) to ONE property w/ its OWN scale & offset. ⊥ a single "reactivity" knob. (4) **layered post, each stage 1 job** — bloom (blur34→level→add), palette (lookup←7-stop ramp), highlight screen, feedback trails, hue drift. (5) **feedback closes on the FINAL output** (`source:"null2"`) ∴ trails carry the GRADED colour, ⊥ the raw render. (6) **a ramp that GOES somewhere** — black→navy→blue→purple→red→gold→white, ⊥ 2 stops. (7) **the palette scale itself is DRIVEN** (highMid) ∴ the ramp BREATHES. (8) **a LONG cycle** — lfo 0.035Hz ≈ 29s on hue. **⚠ CORRECTED (T554): THIS ONE IS INERT.** `hue1.hueoffset` is `unit:"degrees", min -180 max 180`, & `lfoValue` returns `offset + amplitude·wave` **IN THE TARGET'S UNITS** ∴ `amplitude: 0.35` swings **±0.35 DEGREES** ≈ 0.1% of a turn. the PERIOD is right; the amplitude looks ~100× short. the idea is still correct & the FILE never DELIVERED it — ⊥ cite (8) as evidence until T574 lands. ALSO: `valueLag 0.09` between audio & everything ∴ ⊥ jitter (V427,T538) **AMENDED (T544): a gain+bias pair ! be RANGE-CHECKED against its TARGET.** the idiom is right & the owner's own file overshoots `persistence` to 1.57 on a 0…1 parameter ∴ copying the idiom w/o checking the arithmetic ships a clamp
V472: **a REAL-POINTER path can die while ∀ jsdom test stays green.** B107: Radix `SubContent` closes on FOCUS-OUTSIDE, & under React 19.2's focus ordering the pointerdown-driven focus lands BEFORE the flag ∴ the parent sub read its own child's item as outside & closed MID-PRESS — pointerdown hit the item, pointerup hit `<html>`, Radix's pointerup-driven select never fired. the KEYBOARD path worked throughout ∴ every jsdom test passed. ∴ for pointer-driven UI: pin the CAUSE through a path jsdom CAN distinguish (a real `focusin` from outside the React tree; the layer ! SURVIVE) & STATE ∈ the test what jsdom cannot replay (T524,V461)
V473: an exception ∈ the console ⊥ prove a FAILURE — it MAY be a caught one surfaced by instrumentation ∴ read the SURFACE's own report before inferring damage. **BUT ⊥ ASSUME A `try/catch` CAUGHT IT: a SYNCHRONOUS `try/catch` around an ASYNC call catches NOTHING.** `webmcp.ts:142` wraps `context.registerTool?.(tool)` — which returns a PROMISE — ∴ the rejection escapes as `Uncaught (in promise)`. lead read the log & said "partial surface" (wrong); peer read the `try/catch` & said "caught" (also wrong). the LOG ITSELF said `Uncaught (in promise)` & BOTH of us read past it (T550,B108,B116)
V474: **negative space ∈ a Gray-Scott graph = LOWER the black point, ⊥ raise it.** raising `shape`'s black point sends the map to GS's LOW corner & the low corner IS the labyrinth ∴ the frame comes back DENSER, wall-to-wall. the HIGH corner is spots & mitosis — that is where empty field lives. lead's T507 brief said "push toward sparser" w/o naming the direction & the agent pushed the wrong way first (T507,V427)
V477: **∈ a gain+bias mapping the BIAS is the REST STATE & the GAIN is the SWING.** biasing INTO the interesting part of a range leaves nowhere to go ∴ the piece reads as permanently peaking & the audio stops meaning anything — the owner's "always in blast mode". a reactive parameter ! rest LOW & travel; a rest value @ the range's top is a still image w/ jitter. corollary of V471's gain+bias idiom & the OTHER half of T544's range check: fit the span ∈ the range AND put the rest where the eye expects calm (T547,T544,V471)
V478: **a value PRODUCED at N sites & CONSUMED at ZERO is an unenforced property wearing a field's clothes.** `RecompileDecision.recreateTargets` & `resetFeedback` were produced @ 8 sites since T31 & read by NOBODY ∴ V22 ("a resolution change resets feedback") was true only ∈ the type. `resetFeedback` now has its first consumer; `recreateTargets` STILL has none. ∴ gate that ∀ field of a decision record has a reader (T519,V22,T31,T552)
V479 (amends V468): re-check a foreign hunk's imports **AT THE MOMENT OF COMMIT**, ⊥ when you review the diff. `git commit -- <path>` takes the WORKING TREE, & another session refactored the file between the review & the commit ∴ HEAD stopped typechecking. the invariant was ONE DAY OLD & caught its own author (T519,V468)
V480: comparing a structure BY OBJECT REFERENCE makes a correctness bug look FIXED BY ACCIDENT. a JSON parse mints new objects ∴ every edge differs ∴ a cross-document load classified `topology` & nobody saw `NOTHING_CHANGED` — while the SAME code answers wrongly for an edge-less doc ∨ any load path that preserves identity. an accident that hides a bug is worse than the bug (T519,V461)
V481: **FEEDBACK-LOOP DESIGN, measured ∈ E29 over 13 builds.** (a) an EXPANDING loop does ⊥ dim itself — `s > 1` DIVIDES the sampling coords ∴ it DUPLICATES pixels; expansion is ⊥ decay. (b) a ring lit by an ENVELOPE is a **DC term** & a 0.985 loop INTEGRATES it ~60-fold ∴ light it w/ a TRIGGER, ⊥ a level. (c) Contrast inside a loop is POSITIVE feedback; gamma is CONTRACTIVE ∴ reach for gamma — **⚠ SIGN CORRECTED (T598): this catalogue's Level computes `pow(c, 1/gamma1)` ∴ CONTRACTIVE means `gamma1` BELOW 1.** E29's `fade1` carries a comment claiming `gamma1: 1.12` is "below v everywhere"; the SHADER says the opposite. E29 is stable for other reasons — E29's track ! check. these are why "structurally correct" feedback still blows out ∨ freezes (T503,V427)

V483: a module-level `WeakSet` ⊥ guard a page-global registration — **V442's dual module graph gives you 2 Sets**. `document.modelContext` IS identity-stable (probed live: `sameObject: true`) ∴ the WeakSet was sound against StrictMode ALONE & still wrong. the guard belongs ∈ a PAGE-GLOBAL registry: `Symbol.for` on the host document (T550,V467,V442)
V484: **a DOM `select` event ⊥ fire for a caret-ONLY move.** arrow-key repositioning left the completion splice @ the OLD offset ∴ `applyCompletion` inserted the full candidate @ a stale `start`/`end` — the owner's "adds the word in full instead of completing". sync the caret on KEY-UP, & make the fixture CARET-SENSITIVE ∨ it cannot see the bug (T539,B112,V461)
V485: an ASSET reaches the page as BYTES (base64), ⊥ as a PATH — a path is readable only on the HEADLESS twin ∴ a path-taking tool is a ONE-TRANSPORT tool & V39's split. & per V38 putting a file INTO the page is a WRITE ∴ ⊥ export grant (T542,V39,V38,V338)
V486: a SYNTHESIZED preview's source resolution is its CONTENT resolution. a pointset has ⊥ texture ∴ the compiler splats into a target sized by `previewLongEdge` (192) — **BELOW the 384px base tile every preview gets @ dpr 2** ∴ ∀ point & scene-payload preview was UPSCALED AT REST on every retina display, & 6× under boost. the ladder was innocent & so was the content: ink scales with target area (measured 853/24 texels vs an area ratio of exactly 36). size a synthesized target from the BASE TILE (T502,V142)
V487: a preview needs **4 SITES** to agree — the slot, the candidate list, the compiler, the layout model — & each kept its OWN list ∴ `scene` was missing from ALL FOUR & T532's compiler variant alone would have shown NOTHING. **B65 verbatim, 18 months later.** the list lives ONCE ∈ `domain/graph/previewable.ts` & a gate asserts ⊥ site compares a port kind against a preview-only kind ∈ code. + `SCENE_PAYLOAD_KINDS` tied to its union by 2 `satisfies` ∴ a 5th kind ⊥ COMPILE until listed (T532,B65,V437)

V489 (strengthens V479): **verify staging is empty IMMEDIATELY BEFORE `git add`, ⊥ once per session.** an agent verified before its 1st commit, & by its 2nd another session had staged 5 files ∴ `git add <own paths>` carried them into the wrong commit. 3rd cross-session commit-contamination incident. ⊥ amend a tip 3 sessions commit into (B89) — record & tell the owner (T501,V479,V468,B89)
V490: **when a shader contradicts the parameter's own DESCRIPTION, the DESCRIPTION is the contract** — it is what the user READ before they typed a number. `ramp.period` has always said "how many times the ramp repeats across its axis" & the shader DIVIDED the coordinate ∴ it MAGNIFIED. that is a BUG, ⊥ undocumented semantics, & the doc decided it. gate periodic EQUALITY (a column ≡ its half-axis translate), which magnification cannot fake, + a running-cycle floor ∴ a flat field ⊥ pass (T556,V288)
V491: a LOAD-DEPENDENT FLAKE is an ATTRIBUTION TAX ∴ fix the CLOCK, ⊥ the hunt. `viewer-fullscreen.test.tsx` mounts the full App & walks 3 real clicks through userEvent's default inter-event delay; under a PARALLEL suite the 5s budget expired INTERMITTENTLY ∴ it read as "someone's regression" & cost **3 separate sessions** an attribution hunt. `delay:null` + a 20s budget. a test that fails only under load teaches everyone to distrust the suite (T551,V469)
V492: **arguments AGAINST option A are ⊥ arguments FOR option B.** T368 hit B111 & REJECTED the fix — `expression-range.ts` exists ∵ of it — & its 3 arguments are ALL SOUND, but they argue against **WRAPPING** & were read as arguing **FOR CLAMPING**. V494 (⚠ LEAD'S OWN RULE): **`git add <path> && git commit` commits EVERYTHING STAGED, ⊥ only what you added.** the lead's SPEC-only commit `90b028a` carried **33 files** — another session's entire T537 change — ∴ that work's rationale is ∈ no commit message. use `git commit -m "…" -- <path>`, which commits ONLY that path from the working tree. 4th cross-session index incident; the INDEX IS SHARED & `git add` is ⊥ a scope (V489,V479,B89) ⟶ **TENSION SETTLED (T727): the rule exists to stop you CARRYING ANOTHER WORKER'S HUNKS ∴ when a PATH-scoped commit WOULD carry them, hunk-scoped staging SERVES the rule rather than defeating it.** T727 used `git apply --cached` to land E38's own hunks while another worker's RED E2/E24 edit sat ∈ the same file; HEAD did ⊥ carry it. **∴ the ban is on the OUTCOME (sweeping), ⊥ on the mechanism — BUT ASK THE LEAD FIRST**: an earlier worker asked & was refused ∵ its case had a clean alternative (wait for the commit), & self-authorising leaves the lead unable to sequence
V495: **`ctx.frameIndex == 0` cannot be a SEEDING signal — it means 2 things.** "the buffers were just cleared" **&** "the timeline lapped to the in point". neither clock alone carries it: seed on `frameIndex==0` → right after a SEEK, WRONG at a LAP (B-class: E9's fountain re-seeds, the owner's complaint); seed on `absFrame==0` → right at a LAP, WRONG after a SEEK (absolute keeps counting through one ∴ the population never re-seeds). ∴ the signal ! be EXPLICIT (`ctx.firstRun`, use-detected like `ctx.pointer`), set by the events that ACTUALLY clear storage. **making the buffers clear (T552) & telling the kernel they cleared (T510) are 2 HALVES, ⊥ alternatives** — T552 alone leaves the lap reset live (T510,T552,V437,T497)
V496: **Δ=0.00 ≠ clock-dead.** E1 was a STATIC SOURCE THROUGH A FIXED LOOP reaching a STEADY STATE — byte-identical from frame 90 on, ⊥ ∵ nothing read a clock but ∵ the loop CONVERGED. a feedback example ! be RE-FED w/ something that keeps changing ∨ it freezes while being structurally correct (T518,T400,B56,V427)
V497 (⚠ extends V51): **a Level's black point is a SUBTRACTION, & `rgba16float` KEEPS the negatives `rgba8unorm` clamps for free.** ∴ `add` composed a glow over a field sitting @ **−5.9** & SUBTRACTED it — the composite came out DARKER than either input (p90 **0.004** vs the glow's own **0.771**) w/ EVERY structural assertion green (V361). the working-format override has a SECOND consequence nobody had stated. clamp (`limit`) before compositing a level-shifted field (T518,V51,V361)
V498: **a gate's own fixture ! be able to FAIL.** T521's liveness fixture uses a MID-GREY checker ∵ on the DEFAULT black/white checker it measured EXACTLY ZERO with the value graph running perfectly — it would have shipped as a green test that checked nothing. & `measure()` HARD-CODES `animate:true` w/ ⊥ option to disable, & a fixture whose ONLY motion is a value chain runs FIRST ∴ a mis-run reddens the HARNESS before it judges CONTENT (T521,V461,V470)
V499: **count, ⊥ pick.** T546's N-renderer rule: 0 → stock scene; exactly 1 → alias THAT renderer's output; >1 → stock scene **+ an `info` naming EVERY renderer**. taking the first would be a plausible picture from a viewpoint NOBODY CHOSE that silently FLIPS ON A RENAME. & the alias is V130's mechanism (same id, same pixels) ∴ cost is **NEGATIVE** — the 1-renderer case now allocates ⊥ synthesized target (~1.15 MiB w/ depth) & ⊥ draw pass (T546,V130,V109)
V500: **an unfalsifiable guard reads as protection & is ⊥.** T546's sensitivity found a `materialized.has()` check whose removal reddened NOTHING — it could never fail. removed & documented rather than left standing. ∴ ∀ guard ! have a break that fires; a guard w/ ⊥ failing test is decoration (T546,V461)
V501: **a look-pass capture systematically UNDER-REPORTS any FEEDBACK example.** 2 causes, both pre-existing: persistence is applied PER FRAME ∴ a 24fps render measures ~15% HOTTER than 60fps @ the same wall-clock moment; & a from-black capture never reaches the STEADY STATE a running session shows (E31's 599-frame timeline loops w/ the trail carried across laps). ∴ ⊥ judge "is this too hot" on a from-black Dawn frame ∀ anything w/ a loop ∈ it (T554,V470,V496)
V502: when a consumer's mapping EXACTLY un-does a producer's, the producer's gain & bias **CANCEL & can ⊥ change the look** — it becomes a PURE TRANSPORT. E31's `swell1` (×1.25 +0.68) & the kernel's `clamp((inR-0.68)/1.25)` are now that pair, & every extent decision lives ∈ the KERNEL. state it @ both ends ∨ the next person retunes the transport expecting a result (T554,V471)
V503: **ABSENCE ≠ AN EMPTY BAG.** `{}` is ⊥ the same as unconnected: `valueSwitch` counts CONNECTED inputs ∴ an empty bag is still a BRANCH & muting `in1` would silently RENUMBER the rest. ∴ ONE representation — **⊥ channels is SILENCE, whatever produced it**: a mute, a bypassed source, a Switch w/ nothing wired, a stage that threw. silence then propagates TRANSITIVELY: `mute → lag → switch` reaches the switch as an UNCONNECTED PORT, ⊥ as a zero (T541,V329,V457)
V504: **a muted node is ⊥ COOKED** — ⊥ parameter resolution, ⊥ diagnostics, ⊥ state advanced ∴ a `valueLag` RESUMES its trajectory instead of jumping to a value it never travelled to (60 muted frames w/ input 0, then unmute reads ≈0.9355 ⊥ ≈0.017). muting is OFF, ⊥ SILENCED-BUT-RUNNING (T541,T555)
V505: **a CHANNEL is reachable ONLY through `driven` mode, which reads it RAW w/ ⊥ arithmetic around it** ∴ an EXPRESSION cannot see a channel: `floor`/`fract`/`sin` all parse & the scope is `scopeFromFrame`, which holds ⊥ channels. that is why T548 needed a `valueStep` NODE & could ⊥ be an expression — & it is a standing limit on what the value graph can express w/o a node (T548,V107,V453)
V506: **a named reference is a PROMISE.** T528's rule: if `camera` names something, that camera frames the render; if the name ⊥ resolve, the render **REFUSES**; the inline camera is used ONLY when **⊥ name was given**. 1 check covers ∀ 4 failure modes (⊥ such node, ⊥ output, wrong kind, MUTED camera) ∵ the compiler declines to synthesize the edge ∈ all of them. the permissive path was ⊥ load-bearing — 0 shipped examples regressed (T528,V109,V288)
V507: `ctx.firstRun == 1u` **exactly when the pass's storage was created ∨ cleared** — computed per DISPATCH from `freshBufferPairs` ∪ `pendingBufferClear`, both cleared after the submitted frame. use-detected like `ctx.pointer`/`absTime` (V309: a kernel ⊥ naming it emits byte-identical WGSL), group predicate scanned too, & the **SPAWN HOOK reports `usesFirstRun: false` ∵ newborns run MID-SIMULATION** — a point born on frame 900 is ⊥ a fresh buffer. this is the signal V495 said had to exist (T510,V309,V495)
V508: **3 FLAGS, ⊥ 1 overloaded one**: `resetFeedback` (texture history), `documentBoundary` (a LOAD), `{buffers:true}` (point SoA storage). `recompile.ts`'s 4 `resetFeedback` sites — interface, resolution, format, settings — deliberately do **⊥** set `documentBoundary`, asserted BOTH ways ∴ a resolution edit can never rewind the transport ∨ kill a running sim. overloading 1 flag would have made a resize behave like a project open (T552,V478)
V509: **a one-pole LAG ANNIHILATES a single-frame impulse.** E24's `trig1` pulse fed a `valueLag` τ=0.35s, & a one-pole answers a 1-frame impulse with `1−exp(−Δt/τ)` = **0.047** @ 60fps ∴ the palette scale it drove travelled 2.4000 → **2.4535** on a hit. the fast path existed & was DEAD ON ITS OWN TERMS. ∴ a TRIGGER ! ⊥ pass through a smoother sized for an ENVELOPE — give a transient its own short lag (E24 uses 0.04s) ∨ none (T560,V481)
V510: **inject through a THRESHOLD's CUT, ⊥ through a brightness.** a closed gate @ cut 2.0 is EXACTLY ZERO ∵ nothing ∈ a 0..1 field reaches it; a Level shut the same way goes NEGATIVE, & a negative through `screen` is precisely the DC term V481(b) is about. `screen(mask, rd)` takes U & V to 1 where the mask is — literally the kernel's own `seededState` — ∴ `opacity` becomes "how much V a hit drops in" w/ ⊥ extra node (T560,V481,V497)
V511: **V is NEAR-BINARY ∴ a Gray-Scott picture visits exactly TWO stops of any ramp** — which is why E24 was cream-on-navy w/ its OWN blue & teal never on screen. reading the chemistry map a SECOND time, dimmed, & ADDING it to V before the lookup spreads the index across the ramp. a 7-stop palette on a binary index is 5 wasted stops (T560,V471)

V514: **a CONTRACT ! be honoured by EVERY store it covers, ⊥ only the ones that named it.** T552's boundary clear zeroed `bufferPair`s & left PLAIN storage buffers standing, & `spawnFinalize` reads `counts[LIVE]` UNGUARDED — correct against a cold open, wrong against a boundary that had just PROMISED freshness. blanket zeroing is cold-open state ∵ every plain buffer is GPU-written scratch (verified: ⊥ CPU uploads exist ∈ the backend). a half-kept freshness promise is worse than none (T510,T552,V507)

V516: **SCOPE A GUARD TO THE TRIGGER, ⊥ TO THE SYMPTOM.** a veto written against the OBSERVABLE (focus-outside FIRES, Enter ARRIVES, a door OPENS) instead of against the CAUSE it exists for (focus-outside **during a press**, Enter **with a prefix**, **this** door) will be exactly as wide as the observable — & the observable is ALWAYS shared w/ legitimate behaviour. **3 instances**: T524's blanket `onFocusOutside` veto → submenus stacked (B120); T539's 1st Enter gate swallowed Enter for the empty-prefix catalogue; T493's `play` under the lock (caught before shipping). **THE TEST HALF IS PART OF THE RULE**: the fixture ! exercise the LEGITIMATE case the guard could swallow, ⊥ only the bug case it fixes — a guard test that proves only the bug gone passes EQUALLY under the too-wide guard (B120,T524,T539,V461)
V517: **React PORTAL events propagate through the REACT tree, ⊥ the DOM tree.** ∴ a press on a nested menu item reaches EVERY ancestor `SubContent`'s capture handler (the whole branch vetoes — T524's shape) while a press on a SIBLING trigger lives ∈ the parent content's subtree & vetoes nothing. that asymmetry is what makes a press-scoped veto sufficient w/ ⊥ id bookkeeping (B120)

ALSO state the mode it arrives on, asserted (T586,V453,V436)

REPLACE IN PLACE, ⊥ filter-and-append.** `publishParameter` & `exposePort` both appended ∴ re-authoring the first knob's label dropped it to the BOTTOM of the page, & re-labelling a port slid it to the bottom of the node **w/ wires attached**. an edit that reorders is a different operation than the one the user asked for (T423,V80)

V529: **`flattenComponents` is a PURE FN of the document & is recomputed 60×/s.** verified by double-call byte comparison. it costs **5–7× the value graph it feeds** (nested ×50 = **3505 µs = 21% of a frame**), ~60% of that being B41's label-uniquing `structuredClone`. the frame-invariant computation being redone every frame is the ONLY thing here worth a dirty bit (T608,V79)

V531: **DERIVE, NEVER ACCUMULATE — the only hierarchical-time shape that survives V44/V47.** Notch's `Time Stretch` is a pure transform of the parent's time (Speed/Offset/Absolute-Time modes) ∴ an offline render reproduces it exactly. TD's Time COMP `play` LATCH makes local frame a fn of HISTORY ⊥ of the root frame — fine there, fatal here. ∀ future per-subtree time ! be `localFrame = f(parentFrame, rate, offset, start, end, rangeLimit)` w/ every parameter ∈ the DOCUMENT (T608,V44,V47,T431)

V551 (amends V399/T470): **a VANISHED selection CLEARS; it does ⊥ REPOINT.** T470 repointed a missing preset id to Default & left the ARRANGEMENT alone ∴ the menu said "Default" while the shell was Classic — the name fell back & the furniture did ⊥ (T589). one rule ∈ `storeFromRecord`: preset ∨ user layout alike, `currentId` → null, tree untouched, menu reads "unsaved arrangement". **an honest ⊥-selection beats a wrong selection** (T589,T470,V399,V338)

V554 (SCOPES V474): **"lower the black point for negative space" is about **E2/E24's CONSTANTS**, ⊥ a property of Gray-Scott.** measured: driving E2's band w/ a 0..1 ramp for **4 800 steps** gives LABYRINTH end to end — **⊥ spot regime & ⊥ dead corner anywhere**. E24's black comes from its **COLOUR INVERSION**, ⊥ from the chemistry. & `F ≥ 4(F+k)²` is the **HOMOGENEOUS STEADY STATE** condition — Gray-Scott's interesting region lives OUTSIDE it ∴ a pattern is ⊥ a fixed point, & citing that inequality as "where V cannot exist" (as T598 did) is wrong. a band ! be measured, ⊥ inherited (T621,V474,T598) ⚠**REFUTED BY MEASUREMENT (T734) — this row's OWN BAND CLAIM IS WRONG.** it records *"labyrinth end to end — ⊥ spot regime & ⊥ dead corner anywhere"*. E2 & E24 share `GRAY_SCOTT_WGSL` **VERBATIM** (feed .028→.042, kill .0545→.0680) ∴ 1 band serves BOTH, & the measured band (ramp 0..1, substeps 20, 2400 steps, 512²) is: 0.00–0.30 dense labyrinth (cover 51→42%) · 0.30–0.55 open labyrinth/worms · **0.55–0.85 REGULAR SPOT LATTICE** (blob-area CV **0.85 → 0.27**) · 0.85–0.90 dying · **>0.90 DEAD** (cover **0.0%**, motion **0.0000**). **& MOTION FALLS MONOTONICALLY ACROSS THE BAND** — .0085 @ 0.2, .0037 @ 0.83, .0000 above 0.90 ∴ **the lattice band IS where the picture goes still**. the METHOD this row prescribes STANDS; its RECORDED RESULT does ⊥
V555: **an animal deposits SPORE, ⊥ SOIL.** a WHITE deposit raises U **&** V ∴ `U·V²` peaks where the herd already is & the flock COLLAPSES TO A POINT (blown-out core @ identical coordinates @ frames 300/900/1500). deposit into ONE channel. & a FIXED spring has a FIXED POINT — gate homing on HUNGER & walk the roost, ∨ the herd parks (T621)
V556: **`level` applies brightness AFTER invert** — `(1−x)·b`, ⊥ `1−b·x` ∴ a mask built that way multiplies the WHOLE simulation & the field dies ∈ a second. & Physarum's FIXED turn beat a proportional controller here: ablating the proportional version moved nothing (1.56× vs 1.71×) (T621)
V557: **AO belongs on the RENDER, ⊥ on a material ∨ a downstream node** — occlusion is a property of a SCENE (how much of the surroundings a point can see GIVEN EVERYTHING ELSE), a material knows only itself ∴ putting it there is a per-material opt-in LIST, & a downstream node would need the depth buffer ON A WIRE, which does ⊥ exist (`depth24plus` is ⊥ sampleable). **assert it over a TWO-geometry scene** — a per-geometry opt-in passes a ONE-geometry test perfectly. & it multiplies AMBIENT + ENVIRONMENT ONLY: a key light arrives from one direction whether ∨ ⊥ the neighbourhood is enclosed (T624,V437)
V558: **an exact-value gate ∀ a MULTIPLIER ! prove it is ⊥ a GLOBAL DIMMER.** T624's V-groove, ⊥ lights, ambient 1.0 ∴ lit = `albedo × occlusion`: mid-wall on the PLANAR face reads **204 w/ AO on AND 204 w/ AO off** — a global dimmer fails THERE & nowhere else — while the CREASE reads **146**. + intensity 0 reproduces the AO-off row byte-for-byte across 37 pixels **while the passes still run** (T624,V461)

V563: **a PIN can kill the thing it protects.** T609's kind-blind `[0]` pinned-preview pick named a VALUE port as the sink ∴ nothing materialised & **V25 pruned the WHOLE INSTANCE** — the pin that exists to keep a preview alive is what removed it. now takes the first PREVIEWABLE exposed output via the one shared list (`isPreviewablePortKind`), & ⊥ previewable output → ⊥ sink, same as a bare value node (T609,V25,V437,V487)
V564: **a rAF-coalesced INPUT is a correctness bug, ⊥ a cadence choice.** `ui/controls/coalesce`'s scheduler flushed a queued parameter value only on the NEXT FRAME — & CDP pointer gestures land on exactly the window that never gets one ∴ **a dragged slider's value stayed PENDING until the tab was next looked at**. 250ms backstop; the frame still wins while rAF runs. reproducing it required stubbing vitest's fake rAF INERT, ∵ the fake is a timer & timers do ⊥ suspend (T634,V560)
V565: **swallow a BURST, ⊥ a TIMER.** T635's double-add is fixed by ignoring `event.detail > 1` — the browser's OWN statement of burst membership — ∴ a double-click adds exactly once while deliberate separate clicks each add one. a debounce would need a timing constant & would eventually eat a slow repeat-add. gesture semantics beat a heuristic (T635)
V566: **a harness ! surface the diagnostics of the LAYER IT DRIVES.** `renderHeadless` returned BACKEND diagnostics only ∴ a `compiler/substeps-refused` warning on `plan.diagnostics` never travelled & 3 builds shipped believing substeps worked. its gate renders **the shipped mistake verbatim** (`substeps: 4` on a loopless feedback) & asserts the exact code, severity & message reach the caller — a gate built from the real error beats one built from an imagined one (T630,V469)
V567: **an ORACLE that ⊥ evaluate the value graph cannot tell "⊥ animation" from "animation it cannot see".** the cook oracle built plans itself, so T593's `bus.attachChannelResolver` had nothing to attach to — mirroring the harness's `animate` path IS the same resolver route one layer down. gate: an LFO driving a circle's softness w/ an **EMPTY script** now yields multiple distinct digests (red before), & the same run twice is byte-identical ∴ ⊥ wall clock smuggled in (V44/V45). **⊥ 26 per-example oracle tests regressed under a SEEING oracle** (T633,T593,V44)
V568: **9 failing specs, 0 product bugs — ∀ FIXTURE DRIFT against DELIBERATE product changes.** measured per spec BEFORE any fix: 3× edge-drag (nodes render ~514×427 since the design system ∴ absolute offsets overlapped nodes — `elementFromPoint` @ the source handle returned the OTHER node — ∨ pushed a handle off-viewport @ y=1162 ∈ a 1000px window); 3× strict-mode from T492's SECOND CodeMirror; 1× tabpanel strict-mode from the pane tree; 1× file-chooser — **`delete window.showOpenFilePicker` removes NOTHING ∵ the pickers live on the Window PROTOTYPE ∈ current Chromium** (assign undefined to shadow), & then the spec met a deliberate unsaved-changes dialog it predates (V549 again, 2 stacked); 1× a "⊥ input sockets" claim T482 made false by giving Render its environment wire. **a long-red suite ⊥ owned is ⊥ evidence of product rot** (T469,V491)
V569: **noise can hide a GREEN.** `shader-errors`' deliberately-FAILING data-loss gate ("a shader edit survives clicking away onto the canvas") **now PASSES** once its selectors were scoped — the product bug it documented was fixed at some point & the strict-mode noise hid it. whoever fixed it never got the credit, & the FAILING-GATE docblock is now a STALE DECLARATION teaching the next reader that green is unexpected (V421). rewrite it to a plain gate description, keeping one line of history (T469,V421,V491)
V570: **F0 is a SCALAR here ∵ the COLOUR is already carried.** `mix(0.04, 1.0, metallic)` × `params.specular.rgb` — which the compiler already sets to `mix(white, baseColor, metallic)` — IS the textbook `mix(vec3(0.04), albedo, metallic)` @ both ends, w/ ⊥ new uniform. **a VECTOR F0 would have washed out a Phong material's AUTHORED specular tint @ grazing**: E28's ground would have gone 0.0375 → 0.15 ∈ red @ the silhouette — a NEW edge glow, the exact hazard the brief warned against. & @ `metallic: 1` the factor is exactly 1 @ EVERY angle ∴ **metals are byte-identical to what shipped** (T632,T428)
V571: **keep roughness OUTSIDE the Fresnel ∴ today's value is the GRAZING CEILING.** ⊥ Lagarde's `max(1-roughness, F0)` fold: unfolded, the env term can only DECREASE & its grazing value is exactly what the surface already carried ∴ ⊥ rough dielectric can develop an edge glow & a rough metal keeps its dimming instead of snapping back to a mirror (`max(spec·(1-r), spec) = spec`) (T632)
V572: **a Blinn-Phong highlight is an AMPLITUDE, ⊥ a reflectance** ∴ Fresnel does ⊥ belong on it. measured: applying it there takes the blast radius from **2 examples to 4**, & E20-Gooeyball's highlight drops p999 0.5964 → 0.1539 — visibly back to the flat putty T428 existed to fix. scaling an un-normalised highlight by 0.04 does ⊥ make it correct, it makes the control mean 4% of what it says (T632,T428)
V573: **the doc-drift gate's FILTER is what makes it usable.** fenced code blocks only, only the `name(type, …)` idiom, & **only where the claimed type is a REAL catalogue type** — that last clause is what separates `T(point)` as maths & `vec3f(...)` as WGSL from `state1(feedback)` as a CLAIM. **0 false positives across 25 docs**, born red on **6 real drifts beyond the 3 found by hand**: E2/E24 naming `state1`/`out1` where the documents name them `state`/`out` unlabeled, & E29/E30 claiming **`limit`** for 4 `valueLimit` nodes — `limit` is the COLOR Limit node ∴ the shorthand pointed readers at the WRONG catalogue entry. fixed ∈ the `.md`s, ⊥ by relabelling documents (0 pixel risk) (T522,V461)
V574: **a scar sweep ! be MEASURED, ⊥ listed from memory.** T535 was recorded as "E3 & E8" & is actually **SIX sites** — every 4D noise w/ `speed≠0` @ `t4d:0`: E2 (broad **&** detail), E3, E7, E20, E27 — while **E8 no longer has the site at all** (T518 rebuilt it w/o a noise subject; the row predated that). a row naming instances rots the moment anyone touches one (T535,V421)
V575: **when a TUNING CONSTANT stands ∈ for a MISSING TERM, the success measure is that the constant GOES HOME — ⊥ merely down.** E33 needed `7.00/9.00` to read as oil w/ only the specular half of an environment; w/ the diffuse half present its operands are back to the AUTHORED `1.00/0.85`. a constant that merely shrinks means the term is helping; a constant that returns means the term was the thing it was standing ∈ for (T636,T632)
V576: **roughness belongs to the SPECULAR half only.** Lambertian irradiance does ⊥ sharpen w/ polish ∴ tying roughness into the diffuse term would RE-DIM exactly the rough dielectrics the term exists to fill. & the sampling normal FACES THE VIEWER (T301's two-sided rule applied to irradiance) (T636,V571)
V577: **V571's grazing ceiling, sharpened: a diffuse fill term cannot create an EDGE GLOW BY CONSTRUCTION** — @ grazing `(1−F) → 0` ∴ it adds NOTHING @ the silhouette. it deliberately brightens dielectric BODIES, which is its whole job, ∴ every affected example was RE-JUDGED rather than assumed safe. "can only dim" & "cannot glow @ the edge" are different guarantees & the 2nd is the one that matters for a fill (T636,V571)
V578: **⊥ read raw `rgba16float` bytes as luma** — the 1st measurement attempt did & produced NON-MONOTONIC garbage. use the real `toRgba8` conversion, & mask against an env-OFF baseline so what you measure is the ENVIRONMENT's contribution rather than the picture (T636,V470)
V579: **a report can be ANOTHER BUG WEARING ITS CLOTHES.** the owner asked for double-click-to-enter, which had SHIPPED & whose gate passed — they tried it on a BASE node that ALSO offered "Dive in" ∈ its menu & concluded the GESTURE was broken. the real defect was the un-gated affordance. ∴ **verify a "missing" feature before rebuilding it** — the rebuild would have shipped a 2nd path & left the actual bug (T639,T602,V549)
V580: **a command w/ ⊥ input can still be UNREACHABLE — & the cause may be FOCUS, ⊥ SELECTION.** `graph.jumpUp` takes `Record<string, never>` ∴ the lead read the stumble as selection gating; it was **the pane losing FOCUS**, so `u` was dead until a canvas click restored it. fix = watch componentPath **TRANSITIONS**: every depth change refocuses the pane, & coming up SELECTS the instance just exited ∴ the next `i` needs ⊥ hunt. a round trip is 2 gestures, ⊥ 5 (T639,V307,V78)
V581: **key a focus effect on the TRANSITION, ⊥ on the path.** an effect keyed naively on the componentPath ARRAY refocuses on EVERY RENDER & steals focus from whatever the user is doing. the negative half — a same-depth re-render is ⊥ a transition & steals nothing — ! be gated too, ∨ the fix becomes a worse bug than the stumble (T639,V461)
V585: **an ASYNC node takes the ANALYZE SHAPE — GPU half inside the plan, CPU half outside it.** `compileGraph` is SYNCHRONOUS & PURE ∴ a node's `compile()` cannot await, allocate ∨ read ambient state. the GPU half is an ORDINARY pass reading an **`external` scratch texture** someone else fills (the seam webcam/movie/text already use — ⊥ new resource kind, ⊥ new GPU→CPU route); the CPU half is fire-and-forget w/ an `inFlight` guard & a synchronous `latest` map. **PENDING never makes the plan structural** — a node w/ ⊥ result renders its texture's LAST CONTENTS, which the backend contract already specifies. & an inference node ! **⊥** declare `sink: true` (Analyze does ∵ it has ⊥ outputs) ∴ ordinary pruning applies & a disconnected model node costs zero & DOWNLOADS NOTHING (T384,V25,V335)
V586: **`frame.mode` is the reproducibility seam & branching on it is ⊥ a V44 violation** — V44 forbids `Date.now`/`performance.now`/rAF & REQUIRES consuming `FrameEvaluationInput`; `mode` IS that input. ∴ **offline BLOCKS** (TD's non-realtime flag exactly, & both offline drivers ALREADY `await` per frame ∴ free) while **live uses the latest result & exposes its AGE**. both modes produce the IDENTICAL plan — only the fill policy differs — so V47's "same graph + same compiler" stays literally true. & the offline CACHE KEY is free: `(document revision, frameIndex, seed)`, ⊥ input bytes, ∵ hashing bytes needs the readback we are avoiding (T384,V44,V47,V329)
V587: **AUTHOR a parameter from a LINEAR measurement, ⊥ a display-encoded one** — V56/V470's family applied to AUTHORING rather than judging. E34's zero-hit build: the noise's range was read through the **Output node** (display-encoded), the carve window set from those numbers, & every ray then marched through a terrain whose heights the FIELD said were elsewhere — ring floating ∈ the void, 0 hits. remeasured linear (true band ≈**0.36–0.61**), window corrected, 120/240 hits @ frame 0. & measured for the record: **perlin4d's 4-harmonic sum spans ≈0.36–0.61 linear**, reconfirming E27's carve lesson by instrument (T641,V56,V470)
V588: **a more generous DEV device HIDES a baseline limit.** each declared point attribute costs a **read+write PAIR** against WebGPU's **baseline of 8 storage buffers** — & the Dawn device's **10** hid the ceiling until the NO-DEVICE compile path enforced 8. six attrs refused, five refused, **four is the whole budget**. E34's bounce carries its first-leg verdict as a **parked position + a derived distance**, ⊥ as carried flags — & its author judged that GENUINELY BETTER than the flag version. compile against the BASELINE, ⊥ against the machine you have (T641,V309)
V589 (V497, 3rd instance): **the SIGNED pipeline ate a whole night scene.** `Level` emits NEGATIVES below its black point, `blur` SPREAD them, & `add` SUBTRACTED the halo from the shot — a night picture sits almost entirely below the threshold ∴ the ENTIRE film went black. E33's `clip(limit)` exists for exactly this & was dropped; restored, w/ the comment saying it is LOAD-BEARING. 3rd time this pairing has bitten (E4, E33, E34) ∴ a level-shifted field feeding a composite WANTS a clamp by default (T641,V497,V51)
V590 (corrects T576's premise, twice): **the stateful side did ⊥ go blank — it FROZE.** a muted `valueEvaluate` node's ring simply stops being pushed & HOLDS the window it had, a **frozen tail that reads as a signal gone quiet** rather than as a node that is off (verified: the Lag's path is unchanged after the mute). ∴ it was 1 question w/ **2 WRONG answers**, ⊥ 1 right & 1 wrong. & **BYPASS is deliberately ⊥ the same question**: a node w/ a coherent passthrough keeps publishing its input's bag ∴ its plot is TRUE & ! survive; only a bypassed node w/ nothing to pass through is silent — the `bypassPassthroughPorts` predicate the value graph & the compiler already splice by (T576,V504,V109)
V591: **`identity is port-scoped, never node-scoped` — & the same wrong key sat 30 lines apart.** T527 fixed a preview matching its compiled output by `nodeId` ALONE & then building the request from THAT row's `portId`; the `facts` map 30 lines up had the same node-scoped key, where LAST-ROW-WINS made the slot state another port's size & format. `compiledOutputs` carries **⊥ ordering contract** ∴ the fixture ! assert under BOTH array orderings (T527,V100,V461)
V592: **a finished subagent's UNCOMMITTED work is an ORPHAN & ⊥ one will claim it.** T649's fix was written by this session's own T615 agent, left dirty when it completed, & then deliberately worked AROUND by 2 other sessions all day — each correctly keeping its commits path-scoped clear of a hunk it did ⊥ write. ∴ **HEAD failed `pnpm typecheck` while EVERY session's working tree passed** (V547 inverted: the tree was green ∵ it carried a fix nobody had landed). the LEAD ! sweep for orphans when an agent reports done — "tree clean except foreign files" ∈ a report is the signal (T649,V547,V489,V538)
V593: **classify a TYPE by what it CAN be, ⊥ by what a document CONFIGURES.** `movieFileIn`/`audioFileIn` stay **`pure`** as types: locked to the timeline they ARE `f(frame)`, & FREE RUN is a per-document PARAMETER fact `freeRunMediaNodes` already reads through `resolveParameters`. classifying the TYPE `external-live` would **condemn the one configuration ∈ the catalogue built specifically to REPRODUCE**. & `text` stays pure ∵ a browser FONT STACK is a **MACHINE fact, ⊥ a wall-clock one** — V47 is about the same graph & the same compiler (T645,V47,V329)
V594: **the least obvious live device is the one w/ ⊥ PERMISSION PROMPT & ⊥ HARDWARE.** T645's classification found 2 nobody had named: `audioIn` (the session's microphone — V353 makes the ABSENCE of a track deterministic, all-zeros & replayed identically, which is presumably WHY ⊥ one looked) &, harder, **`mouse`** — `valueEvaluate({ pointer })` reads wherever the cursor is @ that instant. a device that asks for nothing is a device ⊥ one audits (T645,V476)
V595: **REPLACE the narrower warning, do ⊥ stand a 2nd beside it.** `freeRunRenderWarning` is GONE; `nonReproducibleRenderWarning` returns ONE diagnostic built from one clause PER CAUSE, from ONE call site — & T586's sentence & its fix-suggestion survive word for word, w/ its tests moved & every assertion kept but the code string. 2 warning functions would have been V519's dead channel waiting to happen (T645,T586,V109,V519)
V596: **a RUNAWAY is worse than a STOP.** T648's worst case was ⊥ the value that committed to ZERO — it was `feedbackEcho.persistence` **0.997 displaying as "1.00" & committing 1.0**, turning a decaying echo into an ACCUMULATOR. a destroyed value is visible & recoverable; a value nudged across a STABILITY BOUNDARY looks fine & then blows up. rank a round-trip loss by WHAT THE VALUE CONTROLS, ⊥ by the size of the delta (T648,B80)
V597: **FREEZE known debt as a RATCHET that can only SHRINK.** T648's 3rd tier lists the catalogue's **27 remaining lossy defaults** (level.contrast 1→1.04, camera.fov 55→54.4, blur.size 8→7.68, limit.steps 4→4.54…) & asserts **each listed entry is STILL lossy** — so healing one FORCES the list to shrink, & nothing unlisted may join. T567's design call is untouched & now owns **a written inventory of exactly what it owes**. a deferred decision w/ a frozen inventory beats both fixing it badly & losing track of it (T648,T567,V541)
V598 (T649 recurring, one message later): **a green `vitest` is ⊥ a green typecheck, & running the gate AFTER committing is running it too late.** `d342f04` landed w/ 3 `TS2339`s — `NumericSpec` carries ⊥ `default` field & the gate read it anyway — while vitest was green throughout. fixed FORWARD (⊥ amend, V489's tree), named ∈ the follow-up's message. **the gate runs BEFORE the commit ∨ it is decoration** (T648,T649,V547)
V599: **a gate that walks ONE SHAPE means nothing for the others.** T648's round-trip walks read `type === "number"` & stopped; a `vector` carries ONE `NumericSpec` for ALL its components ∴ the same derived grid misses every one of them invisibly — **19 of 46 lossy defaults are vector components, incl. inside the ZERO-TOLERANCE starter tier**. a gate reporting zero over half the shapes it claims to cover is V500's decoration (T652,T648,V461,V500)
V600: **the MANIFEST half & the FIELD half are different bugs.** a declared `step` fixes what the manifest says; it does ⊥ reach `beginTextEntry` seeding an input & blur committing that string for a user who typed NOTHING, ∨ `onDoubleClick` running the author's DEFAULT through `normalizeValue`. **guard ∈ `commitText`, ⊥ ∈ the blur handler**, ∨ Enter & blur disagree — Enter on an untouched field destroyed the value identically. & a RESET restores an AUTHOR's number: clamp it, ⊥ quantise it, ∵ it was never a user entry (T652)
V601: **verify a PERIODIC driven value by driving its PHASE, ⊥ by waiting for its clock.** V560 had E31's transport @ 1 frame per FORCED PAINT ∴ a 29-second cycle was unwatchable; stepping `drift.phase` through 0.75/0/0.25 walks the REAL driven path, needs ⊥ clock, & reaches both extremes exactly. restore the phase afterwards (T574,V560,V383)
V602 (V592, other direction): **check your STARTING COMMIT before retracting a finding — the shared tree moves under you.** T652's agent re-probed `Spin`, found it round-tripping cleanly, & nearly concluded it had FABRICATED the bug w/ a bad regex. it checked `d3e69c8` first: `Spin` there is `default: 0.25` w/ **⊥ step**. another session had landed the fix mid-investigation. a TRUE finding was one step from being withdrawn ∵ the ground changed (T652,V592)
V603: **a BYTE-ENCODED payload does ⊥ survive an 8-BIT LINEAR intermediate.** T650 encodes a frame index ∈ row 0's bytes; **display byte 1 is linear 0.0003**, & an `rgba8unorm` intermediate QUANTIZES it to ZERO (the 1st gate run failed exactly there). it survives only through a FLOAT working buffer. same family as V587/V56 from the other side — ⊥ the transfer CURVE this time but the linear intermediate's QUANTIZATION eating a display-precision payload (T650,V587,V56,V470)
V604: **encode the FRAME INDEX into a synthetic source & read it back BYTE-EXACTLY.** T650's test card makes a whole class of broken states falsifiable: "the playhead is `f(frame)`" becomes a MEASUREMENT rather than a hope, an off-by-one is a red test rather than a plausible picture, & salting per `sourceId` means 2 sources cannot silently agree. registering it for every declared `externalTexture` — ⊥ per caller — is what makes every existing gate inherit it (T650,V461,T493,V437)
V605: **a group predicate is ONE DRAW w/ a VERTEX GATE, ⊥ N draws** — `groupMatch(p)` per `instance_index`, excluded instances collapsed to a **zero-area quad** (V219): ⊥ discard, ⊥ indirect rewrite, 0 fragment work, 36 trivial vertex invocations. the lead priced it as a compile-time split & asked whether it needed T481/T624-style parameter pricing; it needs none, ∵ it adds ⊥ NEW COST CLASS. **know the mechanism before pricing the feature** (T642,V219,T481,T624)
V606: **a DEFERRAL ossifies into a RULING when its wording outlives its author's scope.** the scene render's "renderPoints' job for now" was written while SCOPING T428(b) — T428 had shipped surface-only saying "lands with T428", & the message was REWRITTEN when instances landed. **⊥ one ever recorded a reason points cannot draw in-scene**, yet 2 later agents read it as settled & built workarounds around it. a refusal ! say WHY, ∨ the next reader inherits a decision nobody made (T647,T639,V288,V403)
V607: **a predicate that hides a thing ! hide its SHADOW.** an excluded instance still sits @ its REAL POSITION ∴ w/o gating `shadowInstancesWgsl` it keeps DARKENING THE GROUND beneath where it visibly is ⊥ — **a shadow w/ ⊥ caster**. the SAME shared block & the SAME binds go into the shadow generator, & the pipeline gate pins `groupMatch` + the `group_` bind ∈ **every shadow draw**, non-vacuously (shadows FORCED ON ∈ the fixture). ∀ selection mechanism ! be asked what ELSE reads the thing it excludes (T642,V219)
V608: **a TINT is right when BOTH classes are DRAWN & differ only ∈ COLOUR; a PREDICATE is right when one class ! ⊥ be drawn at all.** E34 migrated its ECHOES to a real predicate (they must vanish) & deliberately KEPT its returns/out-of-range split as a tint class (both are drawn, differing only ∈ colour — which is what a tint is FOR). the seam existing does ⊥ make every split a predicate (T642,V471)
V609: **a `$(cat <<EOF)` heredoc w/ an UNQUOTED delimiter runs COMMAND SUBSTITUTION** ∴ zsh ate a backtick-quoted word out of a commit message body ("gains a `group` string parameter" landed as "gains a string parameter"). quote the delimiter — `<<'EOF'` — ∨ backticks & `$(…)` ∈ prose vanish silently. ⊥ amended, per the no-amend rule (T642,B89)
V610: **a camera-facing BILLBOARD casts ⊥ SHADOW — & the reason is 2 layers deep.** a card w/ ⊥ light-facing geometry would cast a shadow that is a LIE; & the sharper half, **w/o an EXPLICIT skip a grid-topology cloud ∈ points mode falls through to the SURFACE shadow branch & casts its MESH's shadow — the ghost of a surface nobody drew.** found by applying V607's general form ("ask what ELSE reads the thing") to the MODE rather than to the exclusion: the shadow loop was reading the mode WRONG. skip stated ∈ place (V403), absence pinned non-vacuously (the light IS casting; the cloud contributes nothing) (T647,V607,V403)
V611: **VERIFY parity rather than claiming it.** T647 needed ⊥ new V487 work — & the reason is worth the line: the preview synthesis has drawn a splat for points-mode geometry **since T532**, & only the RENDER refused ∴ all 4 sites already agreed about everything except the thing that now exists. "⊥ new work needed" is a MEASUREMENT, ⊥ an assumption, & saying which it was is the difference (T647,V487,T532)
V612: **keep a JUDGMENT out of the TIMER DOMAIN & the test's reproduction problem disappears.** T304's verdict is a **pure fn of timestamps** ∴ the gate CONSTRUCTS cadences directly (58fps→live, 1fps+hidden→throttled, 10fps+visible→behind, 31/29 straddling the boundary, stale-frames-outside-window→collapsed) & never has to reproduce rAF suspension — which the debt sweep had to stub vitest's fake rAF INERT to do (V564), ∵ the fake is a timer & timers ⊥ suspend. **the hub REMEMBERS (`recentFrameTimes`), never JUDGES** (T304,V461,V564)
V613: **floor a "too slow" threshold so a DELIBERATE slow piece reads @ its OWN rate.** T304's boundary is **half the PROJECT fps, floored @ 1** ∴ a 2fps art piece reads LIVE rather than BROKEN. a threshold measured against a global constant would have condemned every low-rate document (T304,V541)
V614: **a metrics field that EXPLAINS THE OTHER FIELDS.** `get_runtime_metrics.frameClock`'s docblock tells an agent that **zeros elsewhere ∈ the object are explained here BEFORE they are believed** — which is exactly the misreading T619 fixed & T593's reporter fell into. a reader-facing surface ? carry the reason its other numbers might be lying (T304,T619,V338)
V615: **2 SEAMS CAN PASS EACH OTHER WITHOUT TOUCHING, & an invariant can promise a loop the catalogue cannot close.** V144 exists for image→parameter→image; `analyze` publishes a MEASURED channel; the value graph processes VALUE PORTS; & **⊥ door connects them** — analyze has ⊥ outputs, the driven envelope is unity-gain, expressions cannot see channels. ∴ **every value-processing node ∈ the catalogue is unreachable from the one node whose output most wants processing.** an invariant w/ ⊥ example is ⊥ merely undemonstrated — it may be UNBUILDABLE, & only trying reveals which (T408,V144,V505)
V616: **a WRAPPING parameter as a feedback TARGET flips the loop's SIGN @ the wrap ∴ negative feedback becomes POSITIVE.** measured on E14: error→palette-PHASE tap, phase −0.02 → settled meter 0.51→0.94 ∵ background WRAPS into ramp's white stops ∴ early builds saturated to a WHITE FRAME in 5 frames. loop was correctly signed EVERYWHERE except across the seam @ 0. ∴ ∀ driven param that WRAPS (phase, hue, angle, any `fract`) & sits ∈ a feedback path → its usable range ! be BOUNDED OFF the wrap (E14: bias +0.03, clamp floor 0.005) & bound ! be GATED, ⊥ a comment. corollary: a stability argument computed from LOCAL gain is silent about this — gain says ~0.7 & decays, wrap says diverges|V510
V617: **an UNLIT geometry casts ⊥ SHADOW — ∈ EVERY draw mode.** `materialUnlit` declares a surface takes ⊥ part ∈ lighting ∴ a surface that ignores every light while BLOCKING those same lights is incoherent — it is a reading, an overlay, a marker, a glow sprite, ⊥ MATTER. generalises §V610's billboard argument OFF the mode & ONTO the material ∴ an instanced box under a LAMBERT material still casts (real matter), an unlit one ⊥. completes a symmetry ALREADY half-present: lit shader already declines AO for an unlit model (`ambientOcclusion && options.model !== "unlit"`) ∴ depth sweep now declines to cast, & rule reaches AO sweep too — occlusion is light that FAILS TO ARRIVE & a thing that ! interact w/ light cannot stop it. measured: 2 passes move across 26 examples, both E34 (`shot:shadow:0:1`,`:0:2`), 467→465|V610,T666
V618: **`savePng` ∈ a gpu test writes the LINEAR target ∴ ~1.5 STOPS DARKER than the tile a human sees — ⊥ judge a LOOK from it.** tile always ends DISPLAY-ENCODED (`debug-effects.wgsl.ts`); target declared `linear`. measured on a dark purple PBR material: raw linear median luma **6**, 87% within 12 luma of backdrop = "dark blob"; SAME pixels display-encoded median **38** vs backdrop 66, p95 126, only 7.2% within 12. ∴ ∀ judgement about brightness/contrast/"reads as flat" ← a saved png ! be made on the DISPLAY-ENCODED tile, ⊥ the raw target. 6th reader-that-cannot-see, & this one lies to US ⊥ to the harness — an agent tuning a light rig from these bytes would chase ~1.5 stops that ⊥ exist|V384,V461
V619: **an oracle that picks the FIRST match among STRUCTURALLY IDENTICAL candidates is silently ORDER-DEPENDENT — & it FAILS SILENT, ⊥ loud.** cook oracle's `outputId()` took first plan output w/ portId `"$target"` — but EVERY sink owns a `"$target"` ∴ ∀ doc w/ ≥2 sinks the pick is whatever SORTS first. E14 = first example w/ 2 sinks; meter sorts before output ∴ oracle digested the ANALYZE node's STATIC side texture & read all 80 frames IDENTICAL ∴ **byte-compare PASSED TRIVIALLY** & only the not-a-static-poster floor caught it. ∴ disambiguate by node TYPE (`"output"`), ⊥ by order; & ∀ oracle selecting among candidates ! prove its pick is UNIQUE ∨ name the tiebreak. note the shape: a stricter byte-compare would ⊥ have caught this — the FLOOR did|V461,B127
V620: **"full suite green" ! name WHICH suites.** `src/examples` & `src/tests/headless` are TWO; running one & reporting "full" is §V547's two-tsconfig-projects bug wearing test clothes. E14 shipped w/ headless RED ∵ "full suite 652/652" meant examples only. ∴ a completion claim ! state the suites RUN, & "all tests pass" w/o that list is ⊥ a gate report|V547,V-fail-loud
V621: **our layout gates cover OUR examples ONLY ∴ ∀ change to NODE GEOMETRY reflows USER documents w/ ⊥ gate anywhere.** `examples/layout.test.ts` + §V389 read the 27 shipped files; every user doc was authored against a 100px slot & would reflow SILENTLY, creating overlaps ∈ files we ⊥ own & cannot test. ∴ node-box arithmetic is a COMPAT SURFACE, ⊥ a style knob — changing it needs the same care as an §I change|V389,T668
V622: **preview SLOT aspect FOLLOWS the project's configured resolution — OWNER'S CALL, made w/ costs on the table.** ⚠ REVERSES this row's first decision; measurement below stands, conclusion does ⊥. owner, after seeing the numbers: "make it the default then anyway… follow the configured resolution and thus aspect derived from that… it's fine if we need to manually update or resize them on change. that's what happens in TD too." ∴ manual re-author on aspect change is ACCEPTED COST, ⊥ a bug. **measured cost, unchanged & still true**: 24 of 27 examples are 16:9 & ⊥ move; 3 square projects re-author (E2 3 nodes, E12 1, E24 **28**); @ portrait 9:16 slot = 313px ∴ 25 of 27 violate, 203 overlapping pairs. **∴ §V621 still binds: aspect ! travel as an ARGUMENT from the document's OWN settings (`previewAspectOf`), ⊥ as a constant 2 files agree on** — that is what keeps a user doc's reflow derived ⊥ imposed|T663,T668,V389,V621,V437
V623: **a BLEND whose operand SATURATES deletes its other input — & the deletion is INVISIBLE ∵ downstream still receives a valid field.** E32: `dish1 = screen(coast1, shape1)` & `bowl1`'s background is WHITE ∴ `screen(1, anything) = 1`. measured on the outskirts: **median 0.9989, p90 = p99 = p999 = 0.9989** — chemistry map is ONE VALUE across the whole outskirts, & `terrain1`'s drift is erased THERE by a compositing choice, ⊥ by anything about Gray-Scott. ∴ the "static & homogenic outskirts" the owner saw was a prescribed disturbance ALREADY WIRED & THROWN AWAY. **∴ ∀ blend feeding a field a SIMULATION reads → check the operand's saturating value (`screen`→1, `multiply`→0, `min`/`max`→rails) ∨ a whole region silently loses its input; & gate a driving field by its SPREAD (p90≠p999), ⊥ by its presence**|V554,T657
V624: **⊥ ship a DEAD parameter — wire it ∨ remove it.** a param that drives ⊥ (a value chain w/ ⊥ consumer, a `speed` on a node w/ ⊥ time axis) is a LIE ∈ the document: a reader takes it as intent & an agent takes it as wiring. both E35 cases: `valuemath15`×9→`valuemath16`+1 drives NOTHING while Corona's IDENTICAL pair drives its 3rd renderPoints' `sizePixels`; `noise1` is perlin2d w/ `speed 0.12` & **a 2D noise has ⊥ time axis (T518)** ∴ speed is inert. ∴ decide on PIXELS: wire it & keep it if it earns its place, ELSE delete the param — shipping it dead teaches wrong EITHER way. corollary: an unfinished-intent pair is EVIDENCE (identical precedent ∈ a sibling example) ⊥ licence to invent|T518,B115,V471,T660
V625: **a SAW drives a WALKED angle; a ROTATION wants a SINE @ the same frequency.** a saw's wrap is CONTINUOUS once you take its cosine (an angle the herd walks) & DISCONTINUOUS as a rotation — it SNAPS BACK once a lap. ∴ same clock, same period, different reader ∴ ⊥ re-use the driver, re-shape it|T671,V481
V626: **to break a PATTERN, move the MEDIUM — ⊥ rotate the pattern.** a Gray-Scott lattice needs a STATIONARY substrate ∴ ADVECTION through a STATIC parameter field SHEARS it apart, while a RIGID ROTATION turns the lattice & leaves it a lattice. E32: `displace` between `state1` & the reaction, w/ `pack1` repainting b from `dish1` AFTER the reaction ∴ the field moves & its parameters do ⊥. ⚠ COST, measured: advection eats COVERAGE — @ weight 0.006 dark fraction → 71% & the pasture visibly SHRANK ∵ flow moves V faster than a low-feed regime regrows it ∴ 0.0025 chosen against that; the weight is the density knob|T671,V623,V554 ⟶ **CONFIRMED IN THE WILD & EXTENDED (T734).** ⚠**E24 ALREADY HAD A NODE ∈ THIS SLOT DOING THE WRONG THING**: `wind1`, a `transform` w/ `r: 0.02` between `state` & `rd` — **a RIGID ROTATION applied 17–24× PER FRAME**, precisely what this row says turns a lattice & leaves it a lattice. swapping it for ADVECTION (`displace` from a slow 2-channel perlin, `mono` OFF) **beats the rotation on motion @ EVERY age & multiplies live spot count 2–4×** (f1800: motion .0462 → **.0624**, spots 238 → **907**). **& the COST curve TURNS OVER**: on E2 (substeps 20 ∴ E32's .0025/frame is **.000125** here) dark rises MONOTONICALLY as recorded, **but MOTION PEAKS ~.00025 & FALLS above ~.0005** — past that **the flow REMOVES THE MATERIAL THAT WAS DOING THE MOVING** ∴ the weight has a measurable OPTIMUM, ⊥ only a ceiling
V627: **ADDITIVE point density is RESOLUTION-DEPENDENT ∴ a look call @ HALF RES LIES.** same point count into half the pixels = ~4× the per-texel accumulation ∴ a half-res render BLOWS OUT where the full-res one is correct (found shipping E35). ∴ ∀ look judgement on an additive-points document ! be made @ the project's FULL resolution, & a preview tile is ⊥ evidence about the OUTPUT's exposure. pairs w/ §V618: wrong ENCODING & wrong RESOLUTION are 2 separate ways a saved frame misreports a look|V618,T660,V471
V628: **PREVIEW tiles force `a = 1.0` ∴ they are STRUCTURALLY BLIND to source alpha ∴ ⊥ preview gate can ever verify the VIEWER's compositing.** every lens shader (`debug-effects.wgsl.ts`) writes alpha 1; the viewer BLITS SOURCE alpha (`BLIT_WGSL`) ∴ 6 preview gates stayed GREEN while the product presented BLACK. 7th reader-that-cannot-see. ∴ ∀ claim about PRESENTATION needs a gate on the PRESENTATION path — a preview tile is ⊥ evidence about it (cf §V627: nor about exposure)|V627,V618,T674
V629: **a gate on a DEFAULTED option ! assert the value EXPLICITLY — "! premultiplied" PASSES on `undefined`, which IS the bug.** `ensurePresentation` passed ⊥ `alphaMode` @ all & vgpu defaults `"premultiplied"`; the T87 comment claimed "opaque is CORRECT here" for months ∴ intent lived ONLY ∈ prose (§V478's shape). ∴ read the value off the `configure()` call actually made & assert it EQUALS the intended one|V478,T674,V461
V630: **ALPHA out of [0,1] @ a SINK is a CATALOGUE-WIDE fault class, & "feedback + additive points" is ⊥ the predicate.** measured across 25 shipped: **10** ship a sink whose alpha leaves [0,1]; E12-Fluid & E31-Corona are CLEAN despite having BOTH feedback & additive points ∴ lead's predicate was wrong. real predicate = **sink alpha leaves [0,1]**, & **goes NEGATIVE** for the flicker. mechanism ⊥ unbounded accumulation but a NEGATIVE-GAIN OSCILLATOR: additive passes floor alpha ≈6.02, Screen's alpha map `L ↦ A+L−A·L` has slope `1−A` = −5.02, feedback 0.62 closes it @ gain **−3.11** (measured −3.13) ∴ `M_{n+1} = 6.02 − 3.112·M_n` reproduces every frame. RGB NEVER flickered (luma flat 0.0485)|T674,B129,B130
V631: **a sink alpha's STEADY STATE = per-frame injection ÷ (1 − persistence) ∴ boundedness is decidable W/O RENDERING.** |gain| < 1 → converges (E29: 0.985 → settles 128, flat f300..f800); |gain| > 1 → diverges, & NEGATIVE gain ALTERNATES SIGN (E9: −3.11 → ±f16max). ∴ `persistence: 0.999` settles @ 1000 & `1.0` genuinely diverges. **& ⊥ extrapolate a trend from a SHORT window** — 12 frames of E29 read as "linear & unbounded" & it was CONVERGING|V630,T679
V632: **a display-transform guard ! be scoped to the DECISION, ⊥ to the SHARED SHADER.** `sinkDisplayTransform` returns `toneMap:"none", encode:false` from **3** branches — a `data` target, a `displayTransform:"none"` measurement dump, & an `-srgb` display sink — & all 3 landed on ONE `OUTPUT_PASSTHROUGH_WGSL`. clamping THERE would have bounded the 2 cases that exist PRECISELY so raw numbers read back (§V516 too-wide guard). ∴ `clampAlpha` is a FIELD on `SinkDisplayTransform`, true only for a DISPLAY sink; `-srgb` gets its own shader|V516,V619,T678
V633: **CHROME THAT ! BE LEGIBLE OVER A LIVE TILE CANNOT LIVE INSIDE THE TILE'S RECT.** ⊥ a z-index bug — STRUCTURAL: `.react-flow__viewport` sets `z-index: 2` on a TRANSFORMED element ∴ it IS a stacking context ∴ EVERY descendant is capped @ 2 whatever number it carries, & `.previewSurface` composites tiles @ 30. ∴ ⊥ local fix exists: lifting the viewport above 30 hole-punches (design note rejects it & it would put EDGES over tiles), & a per-frame screen-space overlay is forbidden by §V111. ∴ the control moves to the node HEADER beside P/B/M — OUTSIDE the composited rect|T675,V487,V111
V634: **`elementFromPoint` is BLIND to a `pointer-events: none` overlay ∴ it is ⊥ a gate for OCCLUSION.** hit testing SKIPS such a surface ∴ it returns the button IDENTICALLY before & after the fix. ⚠ LEAD ERRATUM: lead proposed exactly this gate. ∴ gate occlusion by CONTAINMENT/geometry — is the control a descendant of the rect the tile composites at — & verify red-first by rendering the control BACK inside the slot (did: 3 of 4 red, green on restore). general: **a gate that shares the bug's own blindness cannot see the bug** — cf §V628 (preview tiles force alpha 1) & §V629 (asserting "! premultiplied" passes on undefined)|V461,V628,V629,T675
V635: **⊥ pay for a fix by BLINDING THE INSTRUMENT THAT FOUND THE BUG.** T682 would have made `pixel-probe`'s alpha field & `describe_output`'s `channels.a` read a constant ∴ removing the diagnostic that diagnosed T674, to fix a symptom §B131 shows was ALREADY fixed elsewhere. **& the tell that it was wrong: `presentation-alpha.gpu.test.ts`'s range assertions STILL PASS on a constant 1.0** ∴ the suite stays GREEN while ⊥ longer testing what it NAMES — a gate whose subject has been made constant is a gate that has quietly stopped gating|V461,V628,T682
V636: **E1-Feedback-Echo & E12-Fluid carry STRUCTURED coverage ∈ exported alpha** (corr 0.96–0.99; 97.2% of E1's exported alpha bytes ∈ 0–31 w/ real spread above) while everything else is UNIFORM RESIDUE (`aSD` EXACTLY 0.0000 across 921,600 px) ∴ "sink alpha is meaningless" is FALSE for 2 shipped examples. that distinction is MEASURABLE & ! be re-measured before any change to sink alpha semantics|T682,V630
V637: **a LIVENESS gate ! measure PER-SECOND MAGNITUDE — "does it move at all" passes @ 1/1000 speed.** E35's whole authored motion was invisible ∵ a COEFFICIENT LIED ABOUT ITS UNIT (`ctx.absTime * 0.001`, a ms assumption; **absTime is SECONDS per the manifest**), & it passed EVERY "it changes over time" check ∵ the change was REAL — merely 1000× too slow. same class as T574's half-degree hue, ALSO ∈ this file, ALSO a unit mismatch. ∴ a floor on magnitude-per-second catches both; a floor on mere change catches NEITHER|T683,T574,V624,T521
V638: **a marker reading a CHAOTIC source ! RE-SAMPLE ONLY WHILE INVISIBLE — sample-and-hold, ⊥ smoothing.** if it adopts a new reading WHILE LIT it TELEPORTS, & a chaotic map re-qualifies METRES away by construction ∴ the churn is BIRTHS, ⊥ the tail ∴ every tail remedy fails: position lag makes it WORSE (a lagged marker smears across MORE pixels), lagged rise ~4.5%, longer decay 39.1%→34.7%. gate the RE-ARM on darkness & the relocation happens where ⊥ eye can see it — a display's PHOSPHOR. E34: green energy −92.2%, hard-flip 39.1% → 5.4%, for ONE line|T681,V626,T658
V639: **⊥ STRAIGHTEN a normal to tame a chaotic reflection — the chaos IS the phenomenon.** E34's echoes exist ∵ shallow rays reflect FORWARD off BACK-SLOPES ∴ blending the hit normal toward +Y sends leg 2 into the sky & the echo count goes to **ZERO** @ k=0.55 & k=0.80. a "smoother" input to a physical reading can DELETE the reading ∴ measure the COUNT, ⊥ only the look|T681,V626
V640: **a TWO-SIDED diffuse term has ⊥ BACK SIDE ∴ a BACK LIGHT is ⊥ a RIM — the rim lives ∈ the ENVIRONMENT's FRESNEL term.** `scene-render.wgsl.ts` computes `lambert = abs(dot(N, toLight))` & `highlight = pow(abs(dot(N,H)), gloss)` — two-sided by T301 ∴ a directional light BEHIND the subject lights camera-facing faces EXACTLY as hard as the ones facing away: a back light is a 2nd KEY wearing a rim's name. measured: one @ 1.60 blew the emblem's light half to WHITE & produced ⊥ EDGE. what DOES rim is T632's `envFresnel`, which rises to 1 @ GRAZING where the reflection vector points away from the camera ∴ **the rim is authored as a bright band on the equirect's HORIZON @ (0.5,0.5)** — where a silhouette samples. ⚠ lead's "add a rim light" brief assumed a renderer we ⊥ have|T301,T632,T673 ⟶ **WORKED EXAMPLE & LIMIT, measured (mean |Δ| luma, band-wired vs unwired, split by a 6px erosion of the object mask): GOO frame — ring 45.8, interior 25.9, room 2.8 ∴ 1.8× harder on the OUTLINE = a RIM. EMBLEM frame — ring 14.5, interior 19.5 ∴ STRONGER IN THE BODY = FILL, ⊥ a rim.** ∵ a FLAT disc facing the camera has almost ⊥ GRAZING surface for a Fresnel term to find ∴ **the environment-band rim is a rim ONLY on CURVED geometry & is inherently UNAVAILABLE on the flat camera-facing surface a graphic emblem end WANTS to be** — & flattening the emblem (the fix for "the morph ! read") makes it LESS rimmable. state it as 2 NUMBERS, ⊥ 1 adjective
V641: **a RECORDED look number ! name the COMMIT it was measured at.** T689 exists ∵ E33's md carried absolutes w/ ⊥ provenance ∴ ⊥ way to tell a REGRESSION from a table STALE SINCE AUTHORING w/o bisecting. w/ a commit stamp, drift is DETECTABLE by re-measure & re-taking is MECHANICAL. corollary that saved this pass: **record DELTAS (before/after measured minutes apart on ONE tree) as well as ABSOLUTES** — deltas survive a moved baseline, absolutes do ⊥; & a number computed ANALYTICALLY from a kernel's own arithmetic (E33's silhouette CV) is immune to both|T689,V421,V618,V627
V642: **a look rework claiming to be NET-NEUTRAL ! MEASURE the round trip — "the re-exposure comes home" was APPROXIMATELY true & ⊥ measured-true.** T632 raised E33's exposure & T636 reverted the NUMBERS exactly, so the document is byte-identical — but the PHYSICAL terms that replaced its hand gain left it 22% dimmer, 46% flatter & 36% less motion. reverting a parameter is ⊥ reverting an EFFECT. ∴ ∀ change that swaps a hand-tuned value for a physical term ! re-measure the examples it touches & STATE the delta, ∨ the revert is a claim ⊥ a fact|T689,T632,T636,V637
V643: **look baselines are ASSERTED, ⊥ documentation — §V421 AMENDED (lead approved T689's recommendation).** §V421 as it stood cost a table that went stale ∈ **9 hours** while every gate stayed green, & E24's survived ONLY ∵ its round-3 pass happened to re-measure it. ∴ ONE machine-readable `look-baselines.json` (motion/range/f0max per example) measured by the LIVENESS instrument ∴ §V618 & §V627 baked in BY CONSTRUCTION; gated w/ a modest band; updated DELIBERATELY ∈ the same commit as look-changing work (T645's named-list pattern) ∴ **a moved baseline is a decision someone MADE ON PURPOSE**. md tables then CITE the baseline ∨ carry their measurement commit (§V641), ⊥ freestanding facts. rationale: today's engine moved 3× & nothing had to answer for the pixels|V421,V641,T645,T690
V644: **an ALBEDO map MULTIPLIES ∴ an ADDITIVE contribution through it ! read 1.0 WHERE UNLIT — ∨ the surface goes BLACK.** E34's impact pool splats returns via `renderPoints` into a 512² map feeding the terrain's albedo, offset by a Level w/ black point **−0.1** & white **0** — which is how you say ADD ONE ∈ a multiplying slot. ⚠ the naive version (unlit texels = 0) multiplies the ground to BLACK everywhere the pool ISN'T ∴ the bug looks like "the light broke the terrain" & is really the identity element|T672,V617,V640
V645: **a COMMENT that CONTRADICTS the manifest is a TRAP, ⊥ a typo — it sets the next editor up to repeat the bug.** E30-Nave's kernel says "absTime is frames-since-transport-start"; **absTime is SECONDS**. counterfactual measured: the shipped 0.052 behaves as eye-tuned SECONDS (the ×60 "frames belief" version washes range 0.57→0.36) ∴ the NUMBER is right & the COMMENT is wrong — & an editor who retunes TRUSTING it reproduces §V637's fault exactly. it is the ONLY place ∈ the catalogue contradicting the manifest's stated contract. cf §V478 (intent living only ∈ prose); this is its INVERSE — prose that LIES while the code is correct|V478,V637,T687
V646: **SEED look baselines on a CLEAN tree — a shared tree's UNCOMMITTED work gets baked in.** T690's seed ran while the Obol rebuild sat uncommitted ∴ it captured POST-rebuild values (0.01256/0.5953/0.8834), ⊥ the pre-rebuild ones T689 measured hours earlier (0.00763/0.397/0.5963). it came out CORRECT ONLY ∵ that work landed UNCHANGED minutes later — **a worker who ABANDONED an in-flight look change would have left a baseline for a picture that NEVER SHIPPED**, & every later run would then measure drift AGAINST A FICTION. ∴ a wholesale re-seed ! be done w/ `git status` clean. corollary: 2 measurements of the SAME example minutes apart can BOTH be right & DISAGREE ∴ §V641's commit stamp is what makes them reconcilable|V641,V643,T690,T689
V647: ⚠**`audioPattern` synthesizes band channels LINEAR while the REAL analyser path is dB-DOMAIN (byte over a −100..−30 dB window) ∴ tuning against the pattern fits a floor that is ROUGHLY THE PATTERN'S SILENCE & ROUGHLY MUSIC'S PEAK.** measured on E32: `low` under music mean **0.89** / p01 **0.72**; under pattern mean **0.25** / p01 **0.12**. consequence: `pace1` sits @ 0.48 of a designed 0.18–0.52 & `gnaw1` @ 3.8 of 2–4 **PERMANENTLY** — the parameter has ⊥ dynamic range left ∴ music LOOKS like a constant. **this is a DOMAIN mismatch, ⊥ a taste gap** ∴ ⊥ bias set can satisfy BOTH the offline gate & the owner's ears while it stands|T686,V554,T504,T508
V648: **to tell a DOMAIN fault from a CALIBRATION gap, find the channel that shares the domain & check it PASSES.** `level` is AMPLITUDE on BOTH paths (analyser = time-domain RMS, pattern = sum of linear envelopes) & fits r=**0.893** w/ 45% residual — ~HALF the band channels'. **the 4 dB-domain channels are EXACTLY the 4 that fail.** had the pattern's linearity been a general calibration gap, `level` would fail WITH them ∴ the control is what makes "it is the domain" a MEASUREMENT ⊥ an inference|V647,T700,V461
V649: **a PERCENTAGE ! carry its POPULATION — T671's blink instrument was measuring 23 PIXELS.** "nucleus thresholded @ display 205" selects **23 px @ frame 900** under the pattern (155 @ f300) ∴ a hard-flip rate quoted to 2 decimals (23.28% → 18.68%) over 23 samples is NOISE wearing precision. under MUSIC the same threshold selects 2,320–3,342 px ∴ **the 2 rounds were never comparable POPULATIONS** & the improvement they recorded is ⊥ refutable ∨ confirmable. ∴ report the N w/ the %, & when a threshold's population moves w/ the ARM, the metric is ⊥ a metric — use a frame-wide ∨ fixed-region measure instead|T671,T686,V461,V641
V650: **AGREEMENT BETWEEN TWO ZEROS IS ⊥ AGREEMENT — a cross-check case ! PROVE IT CARRIES SIGNAL before its agreement counts.** T702: for a TONE, 3 of 4 bands are legitimately **0.0000** ∴ an all-zero browser reading would have matched an all-zero model & looked GREEN, confirming nothing. ∴ ∀ case ∈ a model-vs-reality gate ! assert NON-TRIVIAL content first. this is §V461's shape ∈ its most seductive form: the gate PASSES, the numbers MATCH, & ⊥ claim was tested|V461,V498,T702
V651: **a BAND-AVERAGE instrument UNDER-READS a NARROWBAND signal — DILUTION, ⊥ refutation.** T702's 1st shape assertion used a 60 Hz TONE & measured steps **0.072–0.124** against the predicted 0.143; the tone occupies ONE bin of `low`'s TEN while the other nine sit pinned on the floor rail ∴ the AVERAGE grows slower than the MAPPING does. BROADBAND fills every bin & leaves only the transfer curve. ∴ measure a band's transfer w/ a signal that OCCUPIES the band; & keep the tone case w/ its reason recorded, so a reader measuring 0.10 where the arithmetic says 0.14 FINDS the explanation ⊥ rediscovers it|T702,V650
V652: **choose the §V620 suite list by WHAT THE CHANGE CAN BREAK, ⊥ by habit.** `e3f39dc` changed a VALUE NODE's OUTPUT DOMAIN & ran audio 15/15 + value-mute-bypass 60/60 + typecheck — all green, all irrelevant to the damage. **`liveness.test.ts` reddens on exactly that commit (5 failed | 28 passed vs 33/33 @ parent)** ∴ ONE run would have caught 5 broken examples BEFORE the owner did. ∴ a commit touching a value node's output domain ! run liveness; more generally the suite list is an ARGUMENT about blast radius, ⊥ a ritual|V620,T703,T701
V653: **a GREEN look baseline means "the picture still looks about the same" — it does ⊥ mean "this example was UNAFFECTED".** E30-Nave: `roll1.value1 ← bore1:low` went from a FLAT CONSTANT 0.000 to a live **0.088..0.289** swing — a DEAD parameter came ALIVE — yet motion moved −6.2% & range −5.7%, INSIDE the 10%/0.003 band ∴ green. 6 audioPattern examples, **5 caught, 1 MISSED**. ⊥ a hole ∈ the gate's construction — a LIMIT on what a green row LICENSES you to conclude. ∴ ⊥ cite a green baseline as evidence a SEMANTIC change did ⊥ reach an example|V643,T703,V649
V654: ⚠**§V471's CALIBRATION BAR WAS NEVER VALIDATED AGAINST MUSIC — E31-Corona's OFFLINE PICTURE HAD BEEN LYING.** the bar did ⊥ MOVE w/ T701: under a REAL track Corona's `coat1.scale` was **ALREADY ~2.0** (`grade1 = 0.55 + 2.6·highMid`, music highMid ≈ 0.56) while the linear pattern showed 0.818–0.928. T547's headroom retune was judged OFFLINE against that pattern ∴ **the standard every other example is judged by has never been what the owner SEES w/ music playing.** ∴ Corona needs its own round & it is the FIRST to be judged against a truthful pattern; ⊥ cite §V471 as settled until then|V471,T701,T547,V647 ⚠**AMENDED by a 2nd, INDEPENDENT measurement — the claim as first filed was TOO STRONG.** the retuning worker re-measured & EYEBALLED E31 post-retune: **character INTACT** — corona, white-hot crests, magenta body, palette breathing, all present. ∴ **Corona was ⊥ MISTUNED AS A PICTURE**; its offline NUMBERS shift (motion +21%, 0.065→0.078) ∵ a strike now decays LINEARLY ∈ dB & carries longer @ the top — a TIME-PROFILE change, ⊥ a look change. **what SURVIVES of this row: the offline picture was never VALIDATED against music, & the parameter VALUES differed ~2× between pattern & track.** what does ⊥ survive: that the bar itself is unreliable. ∴ §V471 STANDS; Corona still earns its own round as the 1st judged against a truthful pattern
V655: **a FINITENESS check CANNOT SEE a DEGENERATE BASIS — zero is perfectly finite.** T706's gate, 1st draft: "every matrix entry is finite" **PASSED w/ the guard DELETED**, ∵ `cross([0,1,0],[0,1,0]) = 0` produces a matrix of entirely finite zeros. caught by the agent's OWN red-verify, ⊥ by review. ∴ assert the PROPERTY THE THING IS FOR — the shipped version asserts SEPARATION OF 2 GROUND POINTS ON SCREEN — ⊥ a numerical health check that a collapse satisfies. same family as §V634 (a gate sharing the bug's blindness) & §V650 (agreement between 2 zeros)|V461,V634,V650,T706 ⚠**5th instance, T705**: that agent's OWN first gate draft asserted window + title + host-present — **the exact broken state the owner reported** — & was replaced by asserting a NEW canvas w/ `ownerDocument === child` actually PRESENTED. the family now: §V634 (elementFromPoint blind to the overlay), §V650 (two zeros agreeing), §V655 (finite zeros), §V656 (right count, wrong order), & this
V656: **a COUNT-ONLY assertion passes a REPLACEMENT THAT WIRES WRONG.** T695: w/o insert semantics a drop-replace still counts right — **3 edges in, 3 edges out** — & lands at the END instead of in the replaced slot. the count reading is TRUE & the wiring is WRONG ∴ gate the ORDER, ⊥ the cardinality; red-first on exactly that (count-only passes while order is wrong). 4th member of the family w/ §V634, §V650, §V655|V461,V655,T695
V657: **a test ! MEASURE ITS TARGET AT THE MOMENT OF THE ACTION — ⊥ before it**, when the action itself MOVES the target. e2e `connect()` measured before the press while React Flow AUTO-PANS mid-connection ∴ harmless on a 1-input node & DECISIVE on sockets **16 px** apart — a drop aimed @ `in2#1` arrived as `in2#2` ∴ every drop-replace assertion would have been meaningless WHILE GREEN. fix: wait for React Flow's own `connectingto` & REFUSE to release when it cannot tell which socket it is over — a refusal that immediately exposed a latent ambiguity ∈ `shader-errors.spec.ts`. & `fitAll` now waits for the viewport transform to STOP, which fixed a pre-existing `project-io` FLAKE|V461,V649,T695
V658: **a `ResizeObserver` BELONGS TO THE WINDOW THAT MADE IT ∴ it survives a cross-document move as a DEAD observer.** T705 layer one: the DOCK's observer fired ONCE mid-detach — `clientWidth` 0 → a **1×1 backing store** → the owner's "empty page" — & never fired again. ∴ ∀ per-window resource attached to a MOVABLE element (observers, contexts, media, timers keyed to a document) ! be RE-ARMED per element after adoption, ⊥ carried|V96,T705,V659
V659: **a WebGPU canvas that was CONFIGURED & then ADOPTED cross-document is PERMANENTLY INERT — & it LIES about it.** T705 layer two, measured AFTER fixing layer one: full size, `getContext` still answering, **0.0 mean luma, forever**. ⊥ error, ⊥ exception, ⊥ refusal you can read. **the ONLY escape is a FRESH ELEMENT** — which the float-THEN-load path had accidentally proven by working. ∴ key the canvas on an adoption epoch so React remounts it; & ⊥ trust `getContext` answering as evidence a context is ALIVE|T705,V658,V628
V660: **guard a DEGENERATE state @ the WRITE path too, ⊥ only the READ.** a read-time guard makes the state SURVIVABLE; a write-time guard makes it UNAUTHORABLE.
