V377's rule (assert the intentional gap) applied to a capability rather than a keybinding.
V393: a DEFAULT ∀ a MISSING INPUT turns "I ⊥ KNOW" into a CONFIDENT WRONG CLAIM. T459's plot defaulted an absent frame time to 0 ∴ the playhead ASSERTED phase 0 on a curve that looked entirely alive — the shape TRUE, the position a LIE. now: draw the curve, draw ⊥ MARKER, & show "—" ⊥ a number the node never produced. distinct from V369 (a fallback MASKING a missing input): here the fallback FABRICATES a reading. ∴ ∀ derived display, ask what it shows when its input is ABSENT — & "⊥ nothing" is usually the answer.
V394: a PREDICATE's NAME is ⊥ its BEHAVIOUR — & I asserted one while CITING V316. I told the track to derive the pure/stateful split from `isValueSourceDefinition`; its body is `valueChannel !== undefined || valueEvaluate !== undefined` — the UNION, true ∀ a Lag as readily as an LFO. they READ IT & built `isPureValueSource` instead. 4th premise of mine corrected by measurement today, & the 1 I had the least excuse ∀.
V395: a DISTINCTION ⊥ shipped node EXERCISES is INVISIBLE through the catalogue, & a sensitivity break over it stays GREEN. swapping the split ∀ the union passed TWICE, because ⊥ node declares BOTH hooks ∴ the 2 predicates agree on everything that exists. ∴ CONSTRUCT the discriminating member ∈ the test rather than waiting ∀ it to ship — V321's lesson (content that instantiates ONCE hides the class) applied to a PREDICATE.
V391: **a SIZE MODEL nobody MEASURES is a guess w/ a docstring.** T460's derived node box was pinned against REAL `offsetWidth`/`offsetHeight` ∈ Chrome — 108 instances, 8 examples, EXACT match — & the measurement caught 2 wrong constants (hairlines added ON TOP of stated heights, when `box-sizing: border-box` puts them INSIDE). w/o it the gate goes GREEN while the examples overlap: the bug 1 level up. ∴ ∀ model of what the DOM renders, PIN IT to a measurement & keep the pin repeatable. (⊥ `boundingBox()` — the viewport SCALES; `offsetWidth`/`offsetHeight` is the GRAPH-SPACE box positions are authored ∈.)
V392: a GUTTER is PER-AXIS because the RISKS differ. vertical needs room ∀ a runtime `.message` row (~30px) that appears the moment the compiler complains — a graph LEGAL UNTIL SOMETHING ERRORS is ⊥ laid out. horizontal needs only what an edge & a port label occupy, because width is a CONSTANT & nothing widens a node @ runtime. 1 number ∀ both flagged perfectly readable columns & would have forced churn ∀ ⊥ reason.
V390: a BROKEN TEST HELPER takes a whole SUITE dark & the suite still REPORTS. B77's `openApp` waited on a selector nothing rendered ∴ every e2e spec failed ∀ the same reason, & "e2e is red" became BACKGROUND NOISE rather than a signal — through the exact window when a GEOMETRY change most needed it. ∴ a suite failing WHOLESALE ∀ 1 cause is an OUTAGE, ⊥ a set of failures; triage the SHARED cause 1st, & ⊥ landing is covered by a suite ⊥ one can currently read.
V385: DEGRADE LOUDLY ON READ, NEVER LOSSILY ON WRITE. a back-compat PROJECTION is written only while it is FAITHFUL; the moment the real shape outgrows it (2 viewers ∈ a 5-zone projection) the projection is **CLEARED, ⊥ left stale** — a stale projection is WORSE than an absent one, because an old build silently restores an arrangement the user abandoned & believes it succeeded. absent → the old build falls back to its default, which is VISIBLE.
V386: the COST CLIFF you fear may already be closed by seams you own. N viewers on 1 output was assumed to mean N renders; V64 already supports any number of SURFACES presenting one output (present per canvas, blit per frame, ⊥ readback) ∴ it is N BLITS. viewers on DIFFERENT outputs route through T252's preview-sink set, which ALREADY makes an unwatched target free & a watched one exactly 1 materialization shared app-wide. ∴ inventory the existing seams BEFORE designing new gating — & GATE the claim (2 viewers, 1 output → resource count unchanged).
V383: **a LOOK PASS is a REQUIRED gate ∀ any example, & it catches a class ⊥ test can.** rendered frames, inspected by a human, ⊥ a green suite. 4 instances, each STRUCTURALLY CORRECT & communicating NOTHING: E2's 1st feed/kill band (both ends a labyrinth ∴ the spatial map bought noise & ⊥ variety); E24's kick driving the lookup's ROW on a HORIZONTAL ramp (B71, a perfect no-op) & wind @ 0.22°/ITERATION killing the dish (B73); E20 as flat putty; E25's 1st render — **a floating torus & NO SCREEN**, because render A cleared to UNLIT BLACK & an unlit-black screen ∈ a dark room is INVISIBLE. every wire bound, every draw drawn, every assertion green. ∴ ⊥ example ships w/o someone LOOKING @ it, & the verdict goes ∈ the report.
V384: a RENDER ! paint a BACKGROUND, ⊥ clear to nothing. E25 proved it: an unlit-black surface composited into darkness is indistinguishable from an absent one, & the failure has ⊥ symptom anywhere ∈ the plan. a backdrop pass @ far depth w/ the colour as a VALUE; geometry composes over it & nothing else clears.
V379: AVOID a silent hazard BY CONSTRUCTION rather than GUARDING it. B33 (a storage-buffer limit breach is SILENT — pipeline creation fails, every dispatch no-ops, ⊥ diagnostic anywhere) was to be handled by counting usage against the budget. instead the lit shader carries NAMED vec4 MEMBERS per light: ⊥ storage buffer, ∴ ⊥ cliff to guard & ⊥ guard to get wrong. the best defence against a silent failure is ⊥ entering its domain.
V380: WGSL codegen notes, paid for: **`meta` is a RESERVED KEYWORD**; and **uniform ARRAY members defeat the uniform writer's REFLECTION** — NAMED members are the one shape every reflector agrees on. ∴ generated uniform blocks enumerate named members, & a count stays STRUCTURAL while contents stay VALUES.
V381: SCENE references COUNT ∈ the cycle gate; FEEDBACK references do ⊥. the distinction is the POINT of each: a feedback ref EXISTS to close a loop the DAG forbids, while scene assembly is ACYCLIC & a loop through it is a REAL ERROR. ∴ an exemption belongs to the KIND that needs it, ⊥ to references generally.
V375: a JUSTIFICATION written ∈ a COMMENT is a CLAIM. if ⊥ test covers it, it is an UNVERIFIED claim wearing the clothes of a verified 1 — & it reads as settled to every future maintainer. TWICE ∈ 1 session the author wrote a reason ∈ a comment & a sensitivity break showed nothing checked it (V123's frame count; `H` centring on CONTENT ⊥ origin, where the test passed under BOTH). ∴ writing "because X" ∈ a comment is the moment to ask what would FAIL if ⊥X.
V376: **COUNT OCCURRENCES BEFORE A REPLACE.** a scripted `replace` of `return holder;` hit TWO call sites & turned a lookup into a registrar — 2nd silent duplicate-block edit ∈ 1 day. a replace-all across a file is a mutation whose SCOPE is invisible ∈ the diff you INTENDED. check the count, | anchor on something unique.
V377: a DELIBERATE ABSENCE ∈ an otherwise-transcribed table is INDISTINGUISHABLE from an omission ∴ a future reader RESTORES it. `H`/`h`/`o` are gone from a TD-verified keymap, so `defaults.test.ts` now ASSERTS each is unbound WITH ITS REASON — 3 omissions turned into 3 DECISIONS. ∀ intentional gap ∈ a table that otherwise mirrors a source: assert the gap.
V378: **DEPRECATED ≠ NON-FUNCTIONAL, & a RELEASE DATE is ⊥ a MEASUREMENT.** I blocked T398 on primary-source research — author's deprecation notice, last release 2025-03-22, ⊥ credential ∈ the maintained fork — & concluded the paste-token flow "does ⊥ exist". the owner had ALREADY RUN IT successfully. the research was ACCURATE & the CONCLUSION was wrong: I inferred capability from METADATA where a 10-minute trial was available. ∴ before declaring a thing impossible, TRY IT — this is V206/B39's rule turned on my OWN verdicts, & it cost a day of the owner's actual goal.
V372: **GPU DATA flows on WIRES; SCENE ASSEMBLY flows by NAME.** pointsets & textures — incl material MAP textures, ∴ E25's virtual-screen stays a plain texture edge — are WIRED. which geometry, which camera, which lights, which material are NAMED. 1 line, & every future "port or reference?" question answers itself.
V373: REFERENCES are the AUTHORING surface; PORTS are the PLUMBING. T350 already resolves a name into a SYNTHESIZED REAL EDGE before validation ∴ scene refs are that, GENERALIZED: the node declares real (reference-fed, connect-refused) input ports, the compiler turns names into synthetic edges, & every edge mechanism runs UNCHANGED. 1 resolution mechanism, ⊥ 2 — which is why the ports→references redirect cost almost nothing.
V374: **a PATTERN cannot be kept COHERENT under RENAME.** `geo*` is ⊥ a reference, it is a QUERY: when `geo3` → `blob1` it silently LEAVES the match set, & when a stranger names their node `geoX` it silently JOINS. that is V320's silent-misbind class made into a FEATURE. TD lives w/ it; our entire name-integrity discipline exists ⊥ to. ∴ EXPLICIT token lists, LIST ORDER = draw/light order (user-controlled & deterministic, better than edge-sort), rewrite clause is token-wise replace. patterns ! answer this paragraph 1st.
V370: a GENERIC ENUMERATOR is ⊥ an invoker. the palette runs `run(entry.command)` — a VARIABLE — ∴ it NAMES nothing & cannot make a command-reachability gate vacuous. MEASURED: adding & removing a palette exclusion changed the failing set by EXACTLY ZERO. ∴ a command reachable ONLY from the palette is PRECISELY the bug the gate exists to find, & needs ⊥ special case.
V371: a PLANNED promise nothing SHOWS is a promise to ⊥ one. `ui.findInGraph` left `PLANNED_COMMANDS` when its binding was removed, because that list's rule is that a planned command ! be NAMED by something on screen. the promise lives ∈ the TASK; re-adding the binding is what restores it, & the gate then DEMANDS the entry return.
V368: ⊥ ship an INERT PORT. a declared input nothing honours is B60/V342 ∈ port form — it invites a wire, accepts it, & does nothing. ∴ a deferred feature has ⊥ port at all, & says so ∈ the node's DESCRIPTION; the port arrives WITH the code that reads it.
V369: a FALLBACK ! ⊥ MASK a missing input. render's ambient floor exists so an unlit scene is ⊥ black — but a scene w/ a non-unlit material & ⊥ light ! also DIAGNOSE (V288), else "dim but visible" is the plausible-wrong answer & the user hunts their material instead of their missing light edge.
V367: a test asserting an OVER-APPROXIMATION of an invariant will FALSELY REFUSE a legal new shape. feedback's swap-late test asserted "after every EFFECT" where V22's actual property is "after every PAIR READER" — & the substep region LEGALLY places unrelated downstream effects after the swap, so a correct new feature read as a violation. ∴ when a gate refuses a change you believe is legal, re-derive what the INVARIANT says before weakening the gate: the bug is ⊥ always ∈ the new code, & ⊥ always ∈ the test either — often the test asserts MORE than the rule.
V361: a connection can be STRUCTURALLY PERFECT & PROVABLY INERT. B71's kick was wired, compiled & bound, & drove a coordinate that could ⊥ matter — every structural assertion passes & the effect is exactly ZERO. distinct from V147 (the picture ⊥ move): here the picture moves, the graph is right, & 1 EDGE does nothing. ∴ ∀ a driven parameter, ask what OBSERVABLE would differ if this edge were CUT — & if the answer needs thought, the test ! assert that observable. ASSERTABLE FORM (T442): render the graph w/ the drive LIVE & again w/ it OFF, & demand the pixels DIFFER — the observable made a test rather than a question.
V362: a capability w/ ⊥ EXAMPLE is a capability NOTHING EXERCISES — & the bug it hides is ⊥ ∈ the feature, it is ∈ its INTEGRATION. `cache` had a per-frame bind-group allocation latent since T237, invisible to its own unit & Dawn tests, & the settled-allocation gate caught it the INSTANT an example contained one. examples are ⊥ documentation; they are the only INTEGRATION gate we have (B42, B39, B41, B72 all shipped ∀ this reason).
V357: define a RECORDED CONTRACT by the INTERVAL it describes, ⊥ by how it is currently COMPUTED — then improving the implementation is ⊥ a versioning event. T437's `onsetCount` = rising crossings WITHIN THE FRAME INTERVAL, `onsetMax` = the interval's PEAK. per-frame analysis makes them 0|1 today; a faster hop later reports real multi-event frames & the MEANING is unchanged ∴ ⊥ track version. had they been defined as "what the analyser returned", every fidelity improvement would have invalidated every recording. corollary of V352, & the way OUT of its cost.
V358: an OPTIONAL REGION ! exist even @ its identity value. substeps' loop region is emitted @ count 1, else 1→3 is a STRUCTURAL change & the animator refuses it — the region is STRUCTURE, the count is a VALUE, & collapsing the region when the count happens to be 1 destroys that distinction exactly when someone turns it up.
V354: "honest-absent" stops being honest when the SURFACE EXISTS. a planned command the palette marks unavailable is fine ∀ a feature ⊥ built; the same treatment ∀ a key naming a surface the user can SEE reads as a BROKEN app. ∴ the planned list ! be checked against what SHIPS, ⊥ only against what is registered.
V351: a fully-wired command can still be UNREACHABLE — REGISTRATION, BINDING & HANDLER ∀ correct, & the CONTEXT never matches. V342 said registration ⊥ prove invocability; this is the next layer: DISPATCH ⊥ prove REACHABILITY. a context-scoped binding needs the context to be ENTERABLE — ∴ whatever DECLARES a context ! also be able to HOLD FOCUS, & the gate ! press the key w/ focus where a USER would leave it (the canvas background), ⊥ where the test conveniently puts it.
V352: RECORD the DERIVED value that determined the output, ⊥ the SOURCE that produced it, when the derivation is ⊥ reproducible. replaying PCM would need re-analysis matching the browser `AnalyserNode`'s windowing & FFT BIT-FOR-BIT across engines — a V47 parity promise ⊥ one can keep. the FEATURES are what actually determined the render ∴ recording THEM makes replay exact BY CONSTRUCTION, incl frame COST (audio-driven substeps become a pure function of replayed numbers). COROLLARY: the derivation's SEMANTICS become the recorded CONTRACT — band edges & flux normalisation are pinned w/ exact values, & changing them is a VERSIONING event ∀ every recorded track, ⊥ a tweak.
V353: DETERMINISTIC SILENCE is ALL-ZEROS, ⊥ an ABSENT bag. a missing feature set would DANGLE every driven parameter; zeros keep the whole graph EVALUATING. an offline render w/o a track replays silence — the same silence every run, never re-listening (V329). **the all-zeros rule covers the ABSENT track; a LIVE mic (`audioIn`) during a take is the webcam problem w/ a different sensor — `external-live` per T645 — & is ⊥ made deterministic by this silence, only by RECORDING TO A FILE first.**
V347: a partially-working LOOP is the worst shape of V147 — it MOVES, so every "does it animate?" check passes, & only the RATE is wrong. B64's 3 substeps advanced 2. ∴ ∀ iterated work, assert an EQUATION only real iteration satisfies: **N frames @ K substeps ≡ N×K frames @ 1, to the byte**.
V348: an AESTHETIC property can only be judged by LOOKING, & ! be REDUCED to a measurable proxy before it can be gated. T388's 1st feed/kill band was too NARROW — both ends produced a labyrinth — & ⊥ test could have said so; it was caught by rendering PNGs & looking. the proxy that DID gate it: pin the animated input & count moving pixels (642 of 262144 = a STATIC MAZE). ∴ look 1st, then find the number that would have told you.
V346: a REGRESSION SIGNATURE ! be SEPARABLE — 1 per FACT the effect depends on, ⊥ 1 ∀ the effect. E20's surface survives ∀ 3 INDEPENDENT reasons & each has its own symptom: normal-aligned displacement (else FOLD ⊥ stretch), continuity ∈ space & time (white noise SHREDS it, a 2D noise type FREEZES it), & the seam being a CLAIM ⊥ geometry (delete topology & ⊥ point moves — the ball just shows a SLIT). "it looks wrong" is ⊥ a signature; 3 distinguishable symptoms are.
V345: a GLOBAL REGENERATOR is a CROSS-TRACK HAZARD. `build-examples` rewrites EVERY generated artifact from the CURRENT working tree ∴ a track regenerating ITS OWN example bakes every OTHER track's uncommitted state into shipped files — & the result LOOKS like a legitimate regeneration. ∀ regenerate-all tool ! take a SCOPE, & the default ! be the narrow one.
V344: a PROCESSOR RE-READS its input; it ⊥ INTEGRATE it. E16's flock binds `position` to the UPSTREAM sphere's pair as a fresh ANCHOR every frame, while `offset`/`velocity` live ∈ the kernel's OWN persistent pairs, & `position = anchor + offset`. ∴ 1 node is a PROCESSOR (⊥ drift, the anchor is authoritative) AND a SIMULATION (state accumulates ∈ the offset) — which is V197's mixed ownership doing real work rather than being a rule.
V343: a MISMATCH check is blind to a LONE input that is simply WRONG. comparing inputs to EACH OTHER catches disagreement & ⊥ a REQUIREMENT — a node needing linear & given encoded has nothing to disagree with. ∴ a node whose maths depend on a space ! DECLARE that requirement on its PORT, ⊥ rely on a cross-input comparison. & "absent ≡ linear" (V57) is an assumption the checker cannot verify.
V342: a command being REGISTERED proves ⊥ that it can be INVOKED. `node.rename` had a bus registration, a keybinding, a menu row & an ellipsis promising a prompt, & ⊥ surface anywhere collects the ARGUMENT it needs. ∴ ∀ command taking an argument the user ! supply, the gate ! find the SURFACE that supplies it, ⊥ merely the registration.
V340: IDENTITY ≠ ROLE. fusing them caps the system @ ONE of each & the cap is INVISIBLE until someone wants 2. `PaneId` names WHAT A PANE SHOWS & WHICH PANE IT IS ∈ one value ∴ a 2nd viewer is unrepresentable — ⊥ by a rule, but because there is ⊥ way to SAY it. ∀ singleton union used as a key: ask whether the user will ever want 2.
V339: a DOM-EXISTENCE assertion is ⊥ evidence a thing is VISIBLE. B54 lived ∈ the DOM w/ a real client rect & painted 0 pixels ∀ months, & the test asserting its presence was green the whole time — jsdom paints NOTHING, ∴ ∀ jsdom suite is blind to "renders | ⊥". anything whose defect is PAINTED-OR-NOT needs a GEOMETRIC precondition asserted (the layer's BOX, ⊥ the element) | a real browser. new V220 shape: wired, mounted, ∈ the DOM, & INVISIBLE.
V338: a FEATURE-DETECTED integration that registers NOWHERE is indistinguishable from a BROKEN one, from ∈ the app. `registerWebMcp` returns `{registered, toolCount}` & ⊥ one displays it ∴ the honest answer to "is the agent connected?" is currently unobtainable. ∀ optional integration: SHOW the detection result, ⊥ merely branch on it.
V335: a diagnostic emitted on a path the LIVE editor DISCARDS ⊥ reach a user. the per-frame re-resolve keeps only `.compiled` & drops diagnostics; the structural compile runs @ the ZERO frame where nothing clamps. ∴ a warning reached tests, gates & offline renders & ⊥ ONE person @ a keyboard. ask WHERE a diagnostic is CONSUMED, ⊥ whether it is emitted.
V336: WRAP needs a PERIOD; a manifest declares a RANGE. ±360 is TWO periods wide ∴ "wrap into the declared range" has ⊥ single answer — 370 → 10 | −350, both defensible. ∴ ⊥ silently reinterpret a range as a period; make the author say `mod(x, 360)` & state the intent.
V337: V186b applies to TESTS, ⊥ only docs. `concepts.test.ts` asserted a clamp warns; the code could ⊥ emit that warning; the test passed anyway. a test can encode a claim that was NEVER true — & then it LOOKS like the claim is gated.
V334: a cleanup that closes a SHARED, NAMED resource ! verify it still OWNS it. StrictMode's mount→cleanup→mount, + `window.open` REUSING a window by name, means cleanup A's deferred close lands on mount B's window. ∴ guard by OWNERSHIP TOKEN (⊥ by `closed`, ⊥ by a timer): close ONLY if ⊥ newer mount has claimed the name.
V331: a DERIVED number ∈ prose is gated by ⊥ANYTHING ∴ it ROTS SILENTLY. E8's 2 wrong figures survived because ⊥ test computes them. ∴ either COMPUTE the figure ∈ a test | ⊥ state it. (V206: I put ~0.046 ∈ the spec; measured 0.0376.)
V332: when a CONVENTION changes, the doc written NEXT gets it right & EVERY doc written BEFORE keeps the old claim — & looks authoritative. ∴ a convention change ! SWEEP prior docs, ⊥ merely document itself going forward. E12 explains feedback-by-reference; E1 & E2 still described the wired back-edge that ⊥ exists.
V333: a test asking the WRONG HARNESS reports ABSENCE as truth. the menus' test asked a bare DOMAIN bus whether `component.publishParameter` was live; component commands are registered on the APP bus ∴ a real command since T132 read as PLANNED ∀ months. "⊥ found" ≡ "⊥ there" ONLY if the place you looked is the place it lives.
V328: state the CAPABILITY, ⊥ promise the HARDWARE. a browser cannot address the Apple Neural Engine directly; WebNN → CoreML → ANE is the only route & exists only where implemented. detect & REPORT what is actually available. (V256: I once wrote "desktop adapters offer 10-64+" from nothing — Dawn/Metal offers 10.)
V329: an ASYNC result ∈ a per-frame graph ! expose its STALENESS. a node silently showing a result from 400ms ago is the V147 family again: the picture is plausible & wrong. & OFFLINE/SEEK render ! ⊥ depend on wall-clock arrival — same project, 2 renders, 2 outputs is ⊥ acceptable ∴ block, | declare non-reproducible loudly. **ENUMERATED & derived-against ∈ `domain/render/reproducibility.ts` (T645): `async-cached` = `analyze`; `external-live` = `webcam`, `audioIn`, `mouse` — the last 2 found by THE CLASSIFICATION ITSELF, ⊥ by any report.**
V330: a VIEWER camera is VIEW STATE (V15) ∴ ⊥ reach the document. & its RESET ! derive from CONTENT BOUNDS, ⊥ a constant — a "reset view" tuned on 1 scene strands the user off-screen ∈ every other.
V325: an EXPLICIT name ! be REFUSED on collision, ⊥ SUFFIXED. a caller carrying a label has references written against EXACTLY that label ∴ silently minting `over1_2` re-creates the dangle V324 exists to kill. suffixing is ∀ AUTO-names only. likewise an explicit field holding EMPTY ⊥ mean "auto" — that is a caller mistake & refuses by name.
V326: a CLIPBOARD OUTLIVES its paste ∴ a rewrite over copied nodes ! own FRESH parameter records. same footgun as the detached-component share (B41): rewriting shared records edits the SOURCE of the NEXT paste. ∀ copy-then-rewrite: clone the records 1st.
V327: refs LEAVING a copied selection stay on the ORIGINAL, BY DESIGN — half a chain pasted beside its driver ! still be driven. only INTRA-selection refs follow the copy. the boundary is a DECISION & ! be pinned by its own test, else a later "fix" will rewrite ∀ refs & silently orphan the paste from its inputs.
V323: same CLASS ≠ same MECHANISM. enumerate a class's members by their FAILURE SHAPE, ⊥ by the fix that worked on member #1. V320's survey: flatten & detach COPY labels verbatim (collision), paste DROPS them (rebind-to-original) — inverted mechanisms, 1 class, & the member-1 fix transfers to #2 & ⊥ to #3. ∴ a class fix is ⊥ done until each member is named w/ its own shape.
V324: an op whose IDENTITY is minted @ APPLY time from MUTABLE graph state is ⊥ REPLAYABLE. `addNode`'s auto-name RECLAIMS names deleted nodes held ∴ undo-then-redo can mint DIFFERENT labels & any reference written against the 1st set dangles. ops that create named things ! carry the name.
V322: a MOCK host that executes ⊥ shader ∴ shader TEXT is unvalidated ∀ suite built on it. structural tests ⊥ substitute ∀ COMPILATION. ∀ shipped WGSL ! be built on a real device by some gate, & that gate's SENSITIVITY ! be proven by breaking 1 shader & watching exactly 1 test go red.
V320: if NAMES are the reference currency (V129), then ∀ operation that DUPLICATES a subgraph ! rewrite them. duplicate-then-resolve-by-name = FIRST-WINS silent misbind, ⊥ error — the 2nd copy's refs quietly drive the 1st copy's nodes. applies to flatten/instancing/paste/import alike.
V321: content that instantiates ONCE hides a whole class of duplication bug. "∀ shipped examples pass" is ⊥ evidence ∀ any property that only breaks @ COPY #2. ∴ ∀ duplicable unit, the gate ! instantiate it TWICE.
V318: "the REGISTRAR MODULE is reachable" ⊥ evidence — a barrel `index.ts` makes ∀ sibling module reachable. rule ! be the factory half's: an exported FUNCTION of the registrar ! be REFERENCED by another reachable module. consts ⊥ count (importing `OPEN_SETTINGS_COMMAND` ∀ a label says nothing about registration). PROVE by unwiring & watching guard go red.
V319: enumerate surfaces by the PRIMITIVE they render (`DialogRoot`), ⊥ by NAME SUFFIX. measured: 5 modules where `Pane|Panel|Dialog` suffixes found 4 — the miss was `CommandPalette`, which ⊥ naming convention would ever catch. a rule over CONSTRUCTION catches the surface nobody named conventionally; a rule over names catches only the disciplined.
V316: an invariant stated over a CATEGORY but implemented over one MEMBER silently NARROWS when member #2 appears. V128 said "∀ reference naming that node"; code scanned `bindings.expression` alone, so `driven` channels (V129, same currency) were never covered & ⊥ one noticed for months. ∴ when adding reference kind #N, ⊥ ask "does invariant cover this?" — read what its IMPLEMENTATION enumerates. structure as CLAUSE-PER-KIND, ⊥ special cases, so kind #N+1 ! declare itself.
V317: a deliberate SEVERITY DOWNGRADE makes every adjacent hole SILENT. unattached channel = info ("reserved, ⊥ broken") is CORRECT ∀ Phase-2 & is exactly why B40 shows ⊥ diagnostic. ∴ when downgrading a diagnostic, enumerate what ELSE it was incidentally covering; 2 individually-right decisions compose into a silent failure.
V314: an INHERITED exclusion carries its REASON; re-derive that reason against CURRENT design ⊥ inherit it. T300 excluded renderer predicates because "vertex stage sees only what that renderer binds" — but narrowness was a fact about the EDGE (untyped pairs → auto-bind = guess), ⊥ about vertex stage. V308's typed payload deleted it & ⊥ one noticed. ∴ before honouring past scope-out, ask WHICH FACT made it true & whether that fact still holds.
V315: a PREDICATE referencing ⊥ attribute → REFUSE. gating ∀ points on constant = bypass wearing costume; `1.0 > 0.5` silently drawing everything (| nothing) ∈ plausible-wrong class (V288).
V313: MAPPED COLOURS are LINEAR BY DECLARATION — a point attribute is DATA (V56/V57), ⊥ display-space colour. a `color`-QUALIFIED attribute (V284) is precisely what a future colour-space op would convert & what a SPATIAL transform ! leave alone; that pairing is the qualifier's whole purpose & belongs ∈ its write-up.
V310: **HYSTERESIS, ⊥ SMOOTHING.** a slow decay is still CONTINUOUS MOTION, which is the thing V296 forbids — it makes the breathing slower, ⊥ absent. the range changes ⊥ AT ALL while the window fits (same object, so a caller can compare by identity), refits AT ONCE when it escapes, & quantises OUTWARD to a 1-2-5 step so warm-up — where the window grows a sample @ a time & technically escapes every frame — lands on the same bounds instead of a hundred of them.
V311: **a flag whose MEANING changes needs migration ∈ BOTH directions, & the FALSE case can be the dangerous one.** `preview: true` meant PINNED → becomes `previewPinned`. `preview: false` meant NOT pinned — i.e. the default — & read as a SWITCH it means OFF ∴ every node anyone had ever pinned & unpinned would open w/ its preview DISABLED & a dark slot. dropped, ⊥ carried. the `true` case is the one you think about; the `false` case is the bug report you get next week.
V312: **count the BUG'S SIGNATURE, ⊥ the fix's presence.** per-frame auto-ranging pins BOTH extremes to the box every frame BY CONSTRUCTION ∴ "0 of 40 sampled frames had both extremes pinned" measures the bug's absence directly, ∈ the running app, rather than asserting the new code ran.
V308: the edge PAYLOAD carries the attribute's TYPE — producers declare it, consumers SWIZZLE FROM IT, never from a guess. 3rd payload fact after half & count, & the 1 T287's qualifiers will want there too once transforms arrive. a consumer inferring type from a NAME is the "index as identity" mistake wearing a different hat.
V309: **"costs nothing by existing" is a SUSTAINED property, ⊥ a 1-off.** 4 optional stages now sit on the point kernels & renderer — group, spawn count, hook, map — & w/ none declared the generated WGSL is BYTE-IDENTICAL to what shipped before any of them. established @ T300 & re-checked @ every addition since. it is what makes the family safe to keep growing, & it stops being true the 1st time someone ⊥ check.
 EARNED ITS KEEP THE HOUR IT WAS WRITTEN, against its own author: T364's first draft routed colour through the VERTEX STAGE even unmapped, silently changing the shipped shader's text — every existing `renderPoints` would have structurally recompiled ONCE & ⊥ one would have known why. caught ONLY because the property was a STATED invariant to check rather than a habit. "it stops being true the 1st time somebody ⊥ check" was ⊥ hypothetical.
V307: **an OPENABLE SURFACE is opened by a COMMAND.** every one ∈ this app already is — help, node info, the libraries, the preview lens — EXCEPT the settings dialog, which opens from a `useState` toggle & is therefore unreachable from the palette | a keybinding. making that the RULE does 2 things: it gives every surface 3 doors (button, palette, key) for the price of one (V78), & it turns T356's weak half into a REGISTRY. the guard currently enumerates dialogs & settings by NAMING CONVENTION — measured honestly: 88 exported components, 63 matching ⊥ family, so a broader check gets switched off within a week — & a surface called `ShortcutEditor` slips straight through. against the COMMAND registry there is nothing to slip through.
V305: **BYPASS ! be TYPE-COHERENT.** T250's splice wired "first input → first output" & was never type-checked ∴ a CONVERTER (renderPoints: pointset in, texture out) bypassed to a pointset MARKER sitting ∈ Output's TEXTURE binding — wrong by construction, since a converter has ⊥ input a consumer of its OUTPUT could bind. now: wire the first input whose port KIND matches the first output's; a converter w/ none MUTES, exactly like a bypassed SOURCE, naming the node & the remedy.
V306: an ambiguity ⊥ yet reachable still gets its RULE stated. `map` resolves against the node's FIRST pointset input; the binding takes an optional `port`, REQUIRED @ validation when a node declares more than 1. v1's consumer has one input & the seam is built for the rule anyway — a default chosen when ambiguity is impossible is a default nobody revisits when it becomes possible.
V303: **an UNSCHEDULED tile RETAINS its last frame ∴ "⊥ updating" ! be VISIBLE.** a canvas keeps its last presented pixels (V204), so a preview that stops being scheduled — off, suspended, off-screen, pinned-but-idle — shows a FROZEN PICTURE that is indistinguishable from a working one. the owner's report is exactly this: "preview disabled but shown anyway just ⊥ updating". OFF ! clear | mark the slot; a stale image is worse than an empty one because it makes a false claim.
V304: **the BUTTON & the STATE ! be the same fact.** today `P` reflects the PIN while the preview is on regardless ∴ the control reads "off" & the picture reads "on". ⊥ 2 fields where the user sees 1 control. (examples store ⊥ `ui.preview` @ all — checked — so the meaning change is safe for THEM; a USER document carrying the old pin semantics is the migration to think about.)
V300: **a REVISION BUMP hands every memo a NEW OBJECT even when the content is unchanged.** settings live ∈ the store ∴ an fps edit bumps the revision & the compile memo receives a new graph object though the document is untouched — every naive memo recompiles on that. key the memo on the STRUCTURAL PROJECTION (`structuralSettingsKey`), ⊥ on the settings object. verified by PLAN IDENTITY across 40 fps edits, which an implementation that merely skipped the GPU half would fail.
V301: `randomSeed` is STRUCTURAL, & it looks wrong. ⊥ pipeline depends on a seed — it reaches shaders through the shared frame block — but the PLAN CAPTURES IT @ COMPILE (V45) ∴ classifying it as a rate would make the edit SILENTLY DO NOTHING. a wrong classification that costs a rebuild is recoverable; one that drops the edit is ⊥.
V302: a dialog's ESCAPE ! ⊥ do 2 things @ once. Escape reverted the field AND dismissed the dialog, so the user never SAW the revert — & Radix listens ∈ the CAPTURE phase, so React's `stopPropagation` is too late & `stopImmediatePropagation` on the native event ⊥ reach it either. a half-typed number is what Escape is FOR first.
V298: **a frame-rate cap w/o PHASE CARRY quantizes to display ticks.** vgpu's loop gates on `timestamp - lastFrameMs >= minIntervalMs` & sets `lastFrameMs = timestamp` — ⊥ carry, ⊥ tolerance. on a 120 Hz display ticks land every 8.33 ms, so TWO ticks = 16.66 ms which is LESS than 16.667 ∴ the gate SKIPS & runs on the 3rd @ 25 ms. sub-ms jitter decides which, so the cadence mixes 16.6 & 25 → **exactly the 45-55 fps band the owner reported**. costs the same on EVERY graph because it is ⊥ overhead — it is the cap arithmetic. a 60 Hz display never sees it, which is why it reads as mystery overhead. FIX: gate on `now >= nextDue − tickDelta/2` & advance `nextDue += interval` (resync clamp when far behind), so long-run rate is EXACT because nextDue never absorbs the wobble.
V299: **measure, fix, REMEASURE — w/ the SAME instrument.** the profile that found V298 was taken live ∈ the owner's browser before anything was touched (V286), & the fix is verified by re-running that same probe. a fix validated by a DIFFERENT measurement than the one that found the bug proves the 2 measurements agree, ⊥ that the bug is gone.
V296: **an AUTO-RANGED plot ! ⊥ rescale from the visible window every frame.** a stable sine BREATHES: as the window slides, the visible min|max wobble slightly, the scale follows, & the wave appears to expand & contract. the range is right ∈ principle (V344's reasoning stands — Slope & Math produce arbitrary numbers a 0..1 range flattens) & the IMPLEMENTATION ! be sticky: expand fast, decay slowly, | quantise to nice bounds. a visualisation that moves when the SIGNAL DOES NOT is worse than a wrong fixed range, because it invents motion.
V297: **the preview toggle is ON/OFF, ⊥ a pin.** V28b made `ui.preview` a PIN ("keep previewing while scrolled off screen") & the `P` button toggles that — so a user pressing it sees NOTHING change, because previews are on either way. the owner wants what the label implies: OFF means ⊥ tile, ⊥ GPU work. PINNING is the rarer need & moves to the context menu. this also bears on perf: today there is ⊥ way to stop a node costing anything.
V293: **a wrapping ERROR SCOPE is ⊥ the same as a USEFUL diagnostic — V240 satisfied ∈ letter, ⊥ ∈ spirit.** the webcam's real failure DID reach the hub, ~30×/second, as `backend/frame-error` carrying Dawn's raw text & the suggestion "Check binding counts and formats against device limits" — WRONG ADVICE, ⊥ nodeId, ⊥ sourceId. meanwhile the media-specific `Media upload for source "…" failed` can NEVER fire: `copyExternalImageToTexture` reports on the device's UNCAPTURED path & does ⊥ throw ∴ the `try/catch` around it is structurally incapable of catching it. a net that catches everything & names nothing sends people to the wrong file.
V294: **the TESTED path is the one the product never takes.** `external-texture.gpu.test.ts` proves the upload end-to-end on Dawn — through `bytes` → `queue.writeTexture`, which needs only `COPY_DST`. the PRODUCT uses the `image` → `copyExternalImageToTexture` path, which needs `RENDER_ATTACHMENT` too & which NO headless test can drive, because Node has ⊥ `HTMLVideoElement`. green coverage of the branch nobody runs. when a capability has 2 paths & 1 is untestable headless, say so ∈ the test rather than letting the other path stand for both.
V295: media sources open SEQUENTIALLY today ∴ a pending PERMISSION PROMPT blocks every other media node behind it. a user w/ a webcam & a movie file sees the movie wait on a dialog it has nothing to do with.
V290: **make an invariant STRUCTURAL, ⊥ remembered.** the reference lines live ∈ a viewport-portal layer, ⊥ ∈ React Flow's `edges` array ∴ `onEdgesChange` can never see them & V151's "⊥ a drop target, ⊥ deletable" is a PROPERTY OF WHERE THEY LIVE rather than a rule someone must not forget. a test asserts the edge array stays empty while a line is on screen.
V291: **2 parsers for 1 address format is a bug waiting.** a `driven` slot names `name` | `name:channel`; the value-graph resolver split on the colon & LIVENESS matched the WHOLE address against node names ∴ `mouse1:x` conferred ⊥ liveness — the node read DEAD while visibly moving the picture, & per V154 that same walk feeds PRUNING & COOKING. survived because the format had 2 readers & 1 of them was never asked about the colon.
V292: assert the INVARIANT, ⊥ the formula. the dash|stroke|arrowhead divide by live zoom so they stay constant ∈ SCREEN pixels (a 6px dash @ zoom 0.2 renders @ 1.2px & reads as SOLID — the same as ⊥ drawing a dashed line). the test asserts `dash × scale × zoom` is constant across 0.2→2.5, so a different correct implementation still passes & a wrong one still fails.
V287: a MAP has ⊥ CPU value — it changes what the CONSUMER COMPILES (the shader reads `attribute[index]` instead of a uniform). ∴ `values` carry the RETAINED STATIC (V108's corner square: inspector, zero-frame compile & every non-point consumer keep working), & the resolution grows a PARALLEL `maps: key → {attribute, channel}` the compile context exposes as DATA.
V288: **store anywhere, FAIL LOUD where meaningless.** a map on a Blur's radius is STORABLE (V107) & the consumer that cannot honour it reports a NAMED diagnostic @ compile — ⊥ silently uses the static, which would be a parameter that looks mapped & is not. same for a map naming an attribute the incoming pointset ⊥ CARRY: name the attribute & what the pointset does provide.
V289: a MODE TRANSITION that changes the SHADER INTERFACE is STRUCTURAL. map↔static is a full compile, ⊥ a uniform write — the same class as a generator's cols/rows (V224), stated up front this time rather than discovered by a stale vertex count.
V285: the TEMPORAL link is a REFERENCE, ⊥ an edge. Feedback names its source; `edges` stays acyclic ∴ V4's "cycle legal ⟺ crosses a temporal node" becomes "edges are a DAG, full stop" — a simpler claim & 1 fewer special case ∈ topology. the dashed line (T248) is what makes it VISIBLE, so the relationship is ⊥ hidden by being un-wired.
V286: **fixed per-frame overhead is a UI question before it is a GPU one.** 45-55fps on a TINY network means cost that does ⊥ scale w/ the graph — React re-render churn @ 60Hz (the timeline readout, & now every value-node PLOT), ⊥ pass count. measure WHERE the frame goes before optimising anything; an audit that assumes the GPU is the cost will optimise the half that is already free.
V283: **an existing GATE can BE the migration audit, & it proves a NEGATIVE prose cannot.** fixing `node()` to MERGE rather than replace could have changed any example relying on the broken last-writer-wins — & regenerating produced BYTE-IDENTICAL files, so nothing was. ⊥ claim needed, ⊥ manual audit: the sync gate answered "did this change anything?" exhaustively. companion to V277 (an existing test passing UNTOUCHED); together: when you change a mechanism, the cheapest proof is the gate that already watches its output.
V284: a QUALIFIER is DATA on the schema, ⊥ a magic name. `heading is a direction` reaches `read_points` as a declared fact ∴ an agent inspecting a pointset ⊥ have to KNOW our naming conventions to treat an attribute correctly — which is the "index as identity" mistake avoided 1 layer up.
V280: **running the SAME file on BOTH hosts finds ENVIRONMENT-SPLIT bugs neither host finds alone.** `createRing` read the browser's `GPUTextureUsage` global; Dawn's bindings INSTALL that global so every Dawn test passed, & the mock host has ⊥ ∴ every example refused to build. the bug was invisible to the suite that exercised the feature hardest. use literal spec constants w/ fallbacks, ⊥ ambient globals.
V281: a helper that SPREADS `extra` AFTER base silently DROPS the base. `documents.ts::node()` takes `parameters` & `extra`, & an example needing BOTH (one parameter a slot, the rest plain) loses every base parameter w/ ⊥ warning. when 2 inputs are meant to COMPOSE, MERGE them — a spread is a silent last-writer-wins.
V282: an INDEX nobody generates goes stale. `examples/README` had ⊥ row for E7 before E8-E10 were added. either generate it from the catalogue | check it, the way `sync.test.ts` checks the files themselves.
V278: **a raw CONTROL BYTE makes a file INVISIBLE to grep, & every caller-search then LIES.** `app/graph-pane.tsx` & `domain/parameters/pulse.ts` used a NUL as a string separator ∴ grep classified them BINARY & skipped them silently. the file compiles, its tests pass, & nothing warns. dangerous HERE specifically: `graph-pane.tsx` is the ONLY caller of `NodePreviewSlot` & `renderPreview`, so searching for either returned NOTHING — precisely the evidence someone would use to DELETE the node preview system. escapes now, + a guardrail scanning ∀ source file for raw control bytes outside tab|newline|return. NOTE: the V207 citation audit was grep-based & would have skipped both files.
V279: **a PLOT & the value it drives agree only where the parameter does ⊥ COERCE.** `brightness` declares `min: 0`, so an LFO swinging −1..1 reaches the uniform CLAMPED @ 0 while the channel reads −0.588 — both correct, & it looks EXACTLY like the plot lying. anyone reading a plot against a rendered result will hit this; the test picks a range the parameter accepts so it measures V275 rather than clamping.
V276: **apply a known lesson PREEMPTIVELY to a new operation, ⊥ wait for it to bite again.** `rotate()` only MARKS during encode; the copy runs @ the next frame entry on T311's unconditional seam — because copying mid-encode would archive LAST frame, since the writing draws submit @ frame end on the loop path. that is B19's submission-order lesson (dispatch submits immediately, draws @ frame close) recognised ∈ a NEW op before a black test found it. the catalogue of bugs is worth what we spend re-reading it.
V277: the STRONGEST evidence a restorage preserved semantics is an EXISTING test passing UNTOUCHED. the ring moved from N targets to target+array & Cache's Dawn content test (tap-1 ≈ recent, tap-3 differs) passed w/o edit — a new test written alongside the change would have been written to the new shape & proved less.
V273: **EVERY node shows what it produces — a texture node its picture, a VALUE node its CURVE.** TD draws a CHOP's channel ∈ the node body & that is why a TD network is readable @ a glance: you see the SIGNAL, ⊥ just the wire. our value nodes (LFO, Timer, Constant, Lag, Filter, Slope, Trigger, Mouse, Analyze) render an EMPTY BOX today, so the half of the graph that MOVES is the half you cannot see. a value preview is a rolling plot of the channel over the last N frames + its current number.
V274: an OUTPUT node ! preview too. it is the node whose content the user most wants to see — the final picture — & it is the 1 node currently showing an empty body.
V275: a value preview reads the SAME channel the resolver reads (V61) & is sampled between frames like every other readback (V16/V48). ⊥ a 2nd evaluation of the value graph for display: a plot that disagrees w/ the number driving the parameter is worse than ⊥ plot, & 2 evaluations of a STATEFUL stage would advance it twice per frame.
V272: **an OPTIONAL prop for a REQUIRED capability is how a feature ships dead — & the author cannot see it.** T247's completion took `scope?` & returned NOTHING without it; nothing ∈ the app ever passed one ∴ the menu could ⊥ appear ∈ any parameter, ever, while its own tests (which pass the prop directly) stayed green. 16th instance of V220 & MINE. the commit even DOCUMENTED the silent-off state — "supplying it turns on completion; without it the field still works, it just cannot suggest" — which is the design flaw written down & shipped as a feature note.
∴ if a capability ! work, the prop is REQUIRED (the build breaks until it is wired) | the ABSENT case is USEFUL (here: names w/o live values). ⊥ a 3rd option.
V270: **a reference EDGE is created 2 ways: the reference gets WRITTEN, or a NAME STARTS RESOLVING.** T331 gated only the first. rename does the second (checked AFTER V128's rewrite, so the check sees the graph the document will hold) — & so does **`addNode`**, which is the unobvious half: V129 numbers from what is FREE w/ ⊥ memory of what still references a DELETED node's name, & `addNode` carries parameters. ∴ a fresh node can be born ALREADY REFERENCED and REFERENCING — both halves of a loop, w/ ⊥ `setParameters` anywhere. clearing a label & deleting a node stay ungated: they only REMOVE edges & already report stranded references (V264 — the gate ⊥ block repair).
V271: **assert the absence of the PLAUSIBLE WRONG ANSWER, ⊥ only the presence of the right one.** decoding a colour @ the read AS WELL AS @ `values` gives `srgbToLinear(0.25)*100 = 5.09` — ⊥ a crash, ⊥ an obvious zero, a plausible number someone TUNES AROUND. that is T187's measured shape, & the range check is what turns it into a failure. a test is worth exactly what it DISCRIMINATES: mutation-check it against the wrong answer you fear, ⊥ only against nothing.
V269: **RULING on `stale` (B36/T341): delete the per-node field; state the PROGRAM-level fact where it is asked for.** V9's staleness is `program !== undefined` — the whole retained program — ∴ true for every node @ once & ⊥ expressible per node. showing 1 program fact on N node badges is the same sentence N times (V90/V92), & the node @ fault is already named by `errorCount`. the POPUP is the genuine counter-case (on-demand, & "is what I'm looking @ stale?" is fair) — so it shows the PROGRAM-level statement, worded as program-level, sourced LIVE from backend status. ⊥ through `publishNodeStatus`, which runs on COMPILE while `backend.status.stale` flips ∈ the frame loop: that wiring would be stale-about-stale. V267 applies — remove the field rather than document that it is always false.
V267: **remove the FIELD rather than document the caveat.** the spawn hook's `Point` deliberately has ⊥ `alive`/`spawnCount`: this frame's lifecycle already ran, so a hook-written kill or birth would be LOST SILENTLY next frame. omitting the fields makes the wrong program fail to COMPILE instead of quietly doing nothing. an ABI that cannot EXPRESS the wrong thing beats one that documents it — a caveat ∈ a docblock is read once & the compiler is consulted every time.
V268: "design w/ <future task>" means LEAVE IT ROOM, ⊥ BLOCK ON IT — unless the row says "after". qualifiers DESCRIBE attributes ∴ Color/Direction/Quaternion semantics attach later w/o touching the hook ABI. blocking a shipped capability on an unstarted design is how both stall.
V265: **when a rejection NAMES the supported value, PARSE THE VERDICT & re-ask** — ⊥ a descending ladder. Dawn refuses w/ "Required limit (64) is greater than the supported limit (10)" ∴ 1 rejection + 1 retry beats 64→32→16→baseline, which overshot a ceiling of 10 on EVERY rung & landed back on 8. cache the proven rung per process so device-loss recovery re-pays nothing. where the API ⊥ name it (browser host), read `adapter.limits` BEFORE requesting & clamp per key.
V266: **a test that ROTS when the product IMPROVES is the wrong test.** the error-net repro hardcoded 9-vs-8; raising the limit made 9 LEGAL & the test would have kept passing while exercising NOTHING. it now builds its overflow against the NEGOTIATED limit (granted+1), following the device instead of a number someone typed.
V257: bind GROUPS ⊥ raise a per-STAGE limit. checked against the spec after checking vgpu's multi-group support — the limit counts bindings visible to a stage regardless of how they are grouped. the instinct "split it across groups" is wrong & would have cost a rewrite to discover.
V258: **spawning MINTS IDENTITY ∴ a schema w/o a u32 id-semantic attribute is REFUSED**, w/ the reason. a spawner that cannot name its children produces points nothing downstream can address (V73) — & the refusal is cheaper than the silence.
V253: a wiring guard resolves IMPORTS, ⊥ matches NAMES. `ViewerPane` existed TWICE w/ different capability ∴ a name-only check sees the mounted one referenced & calls BOTH live — it would have passed straight through B34. the guard found `ShaderEditorPanel` on its 1st run for the same reason.
V254: ⊥ memoise on an object the compiler REBUILDS. the pixel probe keyed its target on the resolved-output OBJECT & `compiled.outputs` is a fresh array every compile ∴ the readout blinked out on every graph edit — which reads as THE PROBE failing, ⊥ as the memo invalidating. key on PRIMITIVES.
V255: folding a feature ⊥ mean folding ALL of it. channel masks|exposure|tonemap did ⊥ move to the presented canvas because V70a says the present blit is a RAW COPY — a display transform there hides which node is wrong & double-encodes once Output does its job. they belong to the PREVIEW path, where `PreviewView` already exists & always passes the default. a refusal stated is worth more than a feature smuggled.
V251: an ABSENT capability limit falls back to the WebGPU BASELINE FLOOR, & that is ⊥ a V12 violation. what needs DISCOVERING is HEADROOM: every conforming device meets the baseline, so treating it as known is knowledge, while exceeding it w/o being told you may is the unsafe act. skipping the check on an unreported key would disable it exactly ∈ headless & CI — the runs w/ ⊥ GPU to complain later.
V252: a PRODUCT budget & a DEVICE capability are 2 different questions w/ 2 different messages, & neither replaces the other. Composite's `MAX_TEXTURE_INPUTS = 8` is HALF the hardware budget by CHOICE (headroom for the frame block; past 8 inputs a graph reads better as 2 nodes). that is ⊥ V213's "2 answers to 1 question" — it is 1 answer each to 2 questions.
V248: **spawn identity uses the GPU COUNTER, ⊥ a hash.** RULING: the hash is stateless & seek-proof by construction, & it COLLIDES — @ 32 bits (WGSL has ⊥ u64) the birthday bound puts 50% collision near ~77k ids, which a particle system reaches ∈ seconds. that is ⊥ "documented odds", it is "will happen", & a collision breaks the ONE guarantee ids exist to give (V73: identity is never a slot). a colliding id is an identity bug you CANNOT SEE. the counter is state that resets w/ the pairs & replays correctly because a seek replays from frame zero (V170).
V249: **alive + spawnCount PACK into one u32 flags attribute** (bit 0 alive, upper bits count). RULING: the alternative — dropping velocity from the default schema to fit the budget — makes the DEFAULT ⊥ the common case & pushes the cost onto every user. packing costs 1 masked read per scan & is invisible outside generated code; `q.alive`/`q.spawnCount` stay SEPARATE fields ∈ the Point struct, codegen packs @ store. coherent because BOTH are write-only (T322's finding). CONDITION: a test ! read BOTH fields back independently after a frame where both are non-trivial (alive=1, spawnCount=5) — a shift|mask error produces PLAUSIBLE output (slightly wrong birth counts), ⊥ a crash, which is this project's worst failure class.
V250: a DROPPED birth is COUNTABLE ∈ v1, even if the diagnostic surface comes later. births past capacity are dropped deterministically from the tail — & a saturating emitter that drops silently looks EXACTLY like a working one that spawns fewer. the count is the cheap half; the surface can follow.
V246: **a data-EGRESS capability is granted by an EXPLICIT act @ the invocation, never by default because the alternative is inconvenient.** RULING on the headless MCP server: `export` requires a flag (`--allow-export` | equivalent); w/o it the tool REFUSES & the refusal NAMES the flag. reasoning, since the counter-case was decent: "launching the server IS the consent act" proves too much — it would justify granting EVERY class. & "the MCP host mediates tool approval" is true & is a DIFFERENT LAYER: it gates which tools RUN, ⊥ what our capability model PERMITS, so leaning on it makes our guarantee depend on someone else's UI. the deciding asymmetry is failure mode: default-ON fails SILENTLY (pixels leave & ⊥ one is told), default-OFF fails LOUDLY & the message is the documentation. cost is 1 line of docs.
V247: **a guard catching its AUTHOR is the strongest evidence it works.** T306's enumeration flagged `createHeadlessMcpServer` as built-but-unconstructed within minutes of it existing — because `serveStdio` is a PROCESS ENTRY the scan does ⊥ treat as an app root. the fix is an explicit NOT_CONSTRUCTED entry recording WHY, ⊥ widening the scan to guess @ entry points.
V245: when a FIX FAILS ITS OWN REPRO, that is the diagnosis sharpening, ⊥ a setback. T327's 1st draft (vgpu `onError` alone) still produced ZERO diagnostics against the literal B33 repro — which is how "vgpu never forwards uncaptured errors" got found. a repro written as the LITERAL bug (9 storage buffers, real backend, Dawn, assert a hub diagnostic) is what makes a failing fix informative instead of confusing.
V243: **a FALLBACK can hide the error exactly where the person is looking.** reading `values[key]` for a cross-node reference looked healthy whenever the binding failed, because V108 hands back a fallback by design. worse for a CYCLE: the loop is caught 1 hop down, THAT hop falls back to its default, & the caller reads the default as a good answer — so the cycle is invisible @ the TOP of the chain, which is precisely where its author is looking. ∴ PROPAGATE the referenced entry's diagnostic, ⊥ its value. generalises: referencing a parameter whose own expression is broken ! say so.
V244: a runtime MITIGATION can remove the pressure to build the real GATE. an `op()` cycle is now NAMED rather than fatal — & V152 asks for rejection @ COMMAND TIME w/ the path named, which still ⊥ exist (`bindCycleDiagnostics` is bind-mode only). file the gate when you ship the guard, | the guard becomes the reason nobody builds it.
V241: **the unwired thing takes a NEW SHAPE each time, so the guard ! grow w/ them.** T306 enumerates `create*`/`open*` FACTORIES; an unmounted React COMPONENT is a different shape & invisible to it. widen NARROWLY where the set is closed & the false-positive rate is low: a PANE is a closed set w/ a registry ∴ "a `*-pane` module nothing renders" is a finding, where "an exported component nothing renders" would drown ∈ noise. the principle: enumerate against a REGISTRY, ⊥ against a naming convention alone.
V242: TWO components w/ the same NAME & different capability is a product decision disguised as a duplicate. `ViewerPane` exists twice: the MOUNTED one (`app/side-panes.tsx`, owns the real canvas) & the UNMOUNTED one (`editor/viewer/viewer-pane.tsx` — channel masks, tonemap toggle, pixel readout, keyboard probe cursor, T36). the app ships the POORER viewer. merging them silently changes what the viewer IS ∴ it wants a ruling, ⊥ a wiring fix.
V240: a driver-level limit breach can produce **⊥ diagnostic @ all**. binding 9 storage buffers against a baseline of 8 made every dispatch silently no-op — plan compiled, frames "rendered", zero errors — because Dawn's validation goes to the device's UNCAPTURED-ERROR path & vgpu's `compute()` ⊥ throw synchronously. ∴ (a) the backend wraps pipeline creation / first dispatch ∈ an ERROR SCOPE & reports through the hub, & (b) the PLAN VALIDATOR counts bindings per pass against `capabilities.limits` & refuses @ compile — which catches it before a GPU exists.
V238: **a test whose NAME states one TRUE claim can PIN a different, FALSE one.** `values.test.ts` asserted `outputs` was EMPTY under the heading "declares no ports and no passes — the number IS the output (§V143)". the heading is true of the COMPILE side (⊥ passes, ⊥ resources, nothing on the GPU) & says nothing about whether the number can be WIRED — so the suite had pinned the missing port AS IF IT WERE THE DESIGN. fix: SPLIT the test, 1 claim each, so neither can cover for the other. this is V237's mechanism sitting inside a test.
V239: when a change gives a node something the COMPILER WALKS (a port, a scratch request, an output), pin what it ! ⊥ cost. 3 value nodes gaining an output port is 3 chances for a Timer to quietly take a full-resolution target — a texture per knob. the guard goes ∈ w/ the change, ⊥ after the leak.
V236: **the pointer is normalised to the OUTPUT VIEWER's rect**, 0..1, v DOWN to match our uv convention. ⊥ window-normalised: wherever the viewer is ⊥ full-window the 2 disagree, & a shader reading `pointer` means "where ∈ the PICTURE". OUTSIDE the viewer it HOLDS its last position — ⊥ zero, because zero is a VALID position & would snap every mouse-driven effect to the corner whenever the cursor crossed into the inspector. ONE publisher (the viewer), per V182.
V237: a value SOURCE needs an output PORT to be reachable from a value EDGE. name-addressing (`driven` → `lfo1`) & edge-wiring are 2 DIFFERENT reachabilities, & T274 delivered only the 1st ∴ `lfo1 → lag1` cannot be wired & Mouse is the only source w/ a port. "single-channel as the degenerate case" was true of the ADDRESSING & silently false of the GRAPH.
V233: a permission CHECK w/o a reachable GRANT path is a permanent denial wearing the costume of a togglable permission — worse than an honest refusal, because it looks negotiable. before adding a gate, verify the grant mechanism is CALLED ∈ the product. (`grants.grant` had ⊥ production caller: 10th instance of V220, fixed inline.)
V234: capabilities gate what affects the PRINCIPAL, ⊥ only what mutates the DOCUMENT. `viewportControl` is the 1st of that class & ⊥ a category error — moving someone's camera costs them their place, which is a real effect on a human even though it touches ⊥ pixels & ⊥ document state. the class will grow (focus, opening a window, starting playback). the alternative — a flat deny for non-human actors — forecloses the legitimate case (an agent laying out a graph & FRAMING it so the human can see the result) & leaves the human ⊥ way to say yes.
V235: a rule is enforced @ the SINGLE MUTATION DOOR (`applyGraphPatch`), ⊥ per adapter. WebMCP, out-of-process MCP & every future transport arrive through it; a rule enforced @ one adapter is a rule the next adapter ! remember — which is exactly the divergence T309 finished paying for.
V230: **the EDGE MAP NAMES THE HALF, ∀ pairs, always. there is ⊥ default & ⊥ convention to remember.** a consumer binds what the payload says — it never infers "write half" | "read half" from what kind of node produced it. RULING on the compacted-pair asymmetry: the inversion is ACCEPTED (compaction lands ∈ the read half, the pair ⊥ swap) but ONLY because the half becomes a PAYLOAD FACT ⊥ a convention. a world where SOME pairs follow a convention & others name a half is the worst of both — a consumer that hardcodes the old rule reads LAST FRAME'S POINTS, which renders as a plausible picture, ⊥ a crash. that is this project's worst failure class (V220's silent-breakage cousin) & the reason the 3rd-buffer alternative was tempting.
V231: an asymmetry is acceptable when it lives ∈ ONE place (the compaction pass + swap placement) & is INVISIBLE to everyone downstream. it is ⊥ acceptable when it is a rule N consumers ! each remember. the test for "which design" is ⊥ elegance, it is HOW MANY PLACES ! know.
V232: v1 of a count-changing kernel is KILL + COMPACT (count may DECREASE). SPAWN needs emitter vocabulary — who authors new points, from where — & is a named follow-up, ⊥ half-designed alongside. renderSurface REFUSES a counted edge (V13): static topology over a varying count is a lie & loud refusal beats a silently wrong surface.
V226: a new RESOURCE KIND should GENERALISE an existing one, ⊥ arrive w/ parallel rules. a frame ring is `pingPong` w/ N instead of 2 — swap becomes rotate, the write target advances 1 slice, reading is a re-point ∴ it inherits V62b's carry, V22's reset & V8's no-allocation-in-loop FOR FREE. the version that arrives as "a 3D texture" (my original wording) needs new answers to ∀ 3.
V227: the alternative someone reaches for FIRST is the one to cost. N targets + copy passes to shift them = N full-frame copies/frame (~1 GB/frame @ 60×1080p write bandwidth); the rotation costs an INTEGER. that comparison IS the argument for a resource kind existing — w/o it "why ⊥ just copy" reopens forever.
V228: when COST is the parameter, the cost is stated ON the node (Blur states its tap budget; a Cache states its bytes). defaults ! be modest: `frames: 8`, `scale: 1/2` ≈ 32 MiB. a dropped node costing 949 MiB — 93% of the default budget — is a trap the library hands you.
V229: a temporal buffer READ BEFORE IT HAS FILLED HOLDS its oldest slice, ⊥ returns black. holding is deterministic & is what "a trail growing in" looks like; black FLASHES on every reset & is the kind of thing that renders fine locally & differs ∈ a headless parity run (V45/V47).
V224: a parameter feeding any PUBLISHED EDGE PAYLOAD is `compileTime` by definition — **the classifier cannot see through an edge**. a generator's cols/rows/shape decide the pointset payload downstream consumers compile against ∴ under T308 a value-only `cols` edit would leave a consuming surface's vertex count STALE. shape still reaches the kernel as a uniform, so the recompile is a V62b cache hit, ⊥ a pipeline swap.
V225: a graph whose ONLY motion is a VALUE CHAIN ⊥ idle-skip (T254). a stateful value stage makes "nothing changed" false every frame even w/ a static document ∴ session evaluation ! feed the gate's inputs, & `hasAnimatedParameters` ! count `driven` mode — a Mouse→param graph has ⊥ expressions & ! still animate.
V221: a SEAM w/ >1 optional rider takes an OPTIONS OBJECT, ⊥ positions. `useFrameLoop` grew to 7 positional params — 3 optional, 2 callbacks & a boolean among them — & a parallel change inserted `valuesOnly` AHEAD of `observe`. tsc caught it only because the types happened to differ; a 2nd CALLBACK would have compiled & silently swapped the riders.
V222: a rider that STOPS RUNNING ! be detectable. a pulse that never fires & a readback that never happens both look EXACTLY like a graph w/ nothing to do ∴ ⊥ rider throws, ⊥ diagnostic appears, & the seam test is the only thing that can tell. test the SEAM (drive 1 real frame, assert the rider ran), ⊥ the signature.
V223: `readBuffer` asserts OUTSIDE-FRAME, & the frame driver's `onFrame` runs INSIDE the open frame (`runFrame` holds the guard closed for the whole callback). ∴ "sample ∈ the between-frames slot beside the uniform push" — which I RELAYED as guidance — would have produced a channel that never updates: `sample()` swallows a failed read by contract (V144, stale beats stalled) ∴ ⊥ error, ⊥ symptom, B25 one layer down. queue it as a MICROTASK from the frame observer; vgpu's `frame()` is synchronous & the guard restores ∈ a `finally`.
V220: **"built, tested, ⊥ wired" is this project's DOMINANT failure mode — 14 instances, ≥4 SHAPES (factory, port, mount, CAPABILITY-w/o-UI).** B12 agent surface, T264 media, B23 render_preview, B25 analyze channels, B26 recompile classifier, `ContextMenuHost` (every menu the menus track built was unreachable while its suite stayed green), `createValueGraphSession` (B27), `createShaderCompilePipeline` (B28), component FLATTENING (B29), `grants.grant` (fixed inline), `PointerSource.set` (B30), `setCookPolicy` (B32). ∀ had green unit tests. the shared cause: a test SUPPLIES the wiring it is testing ∴ the 1 thing it cannot observe is whether anything else supplies it. T306's enumeration is the structural answer & ! outlive the tasks that prompted it.
V215: the agent boundary is narrower than the domain BY POLICY, never by DRIFT. every difference is STATED (agents ⊥ mint ids — `addNode`/`addGroup` w/ a bare ref could collide w/ or impersonate entities they ⊥ create) | it is a bug. 2 unions describing 1 thing produced: 6 ops the agent never got, `ParameterValue` where the domain takes `StoredParameter` (**an agent could write a NUMBER but ⊥ the EXPRESSION that produces one** — a mode V107 promises ∀ parameter), & override validation written twice w/ only 1 copy running.
V216: an optimisation ⊥ delete the ONLY PRODUCER of a diagnostic for exactly the edit that introduces it. a parameter expression w/ valid SYNTAX & an unresolvable NAME is accepted @ patch time & diagnosed ONLY by `compileGraph`. "a value edit ⊥ run compileGraph" would silence it precisely when it fires. same species as V209 — ask what the optimisation REMOVES.
V217: `compileGraph` is the ONLY producer of resolved uniform VALUES. a 2nd producer is a 2nd evaluator (V109) ∴ getting it off the value path means a VALUES-ONLY compile that reuses the previous plan's structure — a real compiler mode, ⊥ an app-layer skip.
V213: where a value is COERCED @ runtime, ⊥ ALSO reject it @ write time — 2 answers to 1 question. Switch's index WRAPS, so a declared min|max would refuse a static `9` (V66) while an expression producing 9 wrapped happily. pick the coercion | pick the validation; shipping both means the same number is legal ∈ 1 mode & illegal ∈ another.
V214: RANGE HANDLING is a product decision, ⊥ a default. Switch WRAPS rather than clamps: clamping turns "cycle through my sources" into "stop on the last one", fixable only by typing a modulo into every driving expression — whereas wrapping leaves clamping available upstream. choose the one the USER can undo.
V211: OBSERVING something ⊥ require the graph to be ANIMATING. T311's preview retry runs BEFORE the idle-skip gate ∴ a fully static plan under `auto` still heals its previews. same spirit as V142 (the camera is free): the cost of LOOKING is ⊥ allowed to depend on whether anything is moving.
V212: a repair that ALLOCATES ⊥ run inside an open frame — the V8 guard refuses allocation once `duringFrame` begins, so a retry placed there trades a blackout for a per-frame THROW. repairs go @ frame ENTRY, before the frame opens.
V207: citation-grep is a LEAD, ⊥ a verdict. audit found 40 of 222 uncited ∈ `src/`, 5 claimed done by a §T row — & the 2 I then checked BY HAND (V62a, V98) are both fully implemented, paraphrased ∈ prose w/o their number. so the metric's false-positive rate is high & a report from it ! be verified before it is believed. what it IS good for: a shortlist. B26 (the unwired classifier) is the shape it hunts, & was found by asking "what CALLS this?" — which is the question worth automating, ⊥ "is the number mentioned".
V207b: there are TWO label namespaces & only 1 is documented. `R4`/`R9` appear ∈ `src/` as recovery-rule labels w/ ⊥ §-section defining them. a 2nd undocumented scheme defeats every grep-based audit & every reader looking for the rule. either fold them into §V | give them a section.
V208: 2 unions describing the SAME thing ! be generated from 1, or a test ! prove them equal. `src/agent/schemas.ts` keeps its OWN patch-op union ∴ `setNodeSize` exists ∈ the domain & ⊥ for an agent, & ⊥ test catches the divergence. it will drift again on the next op.
V205: V193's enumeration covers AGENT TOOLS. that is ⊥ enough — the 4th instance of "built, tested, ⊥ wired" was `createAnalyzeChannels`, a RUNTIME SERVICE constructed only ∈ its own gpu test, so the image→parameter loop is ⊥ closed ∈ the product & no agent-tool guard could see it. the enumeration ! cover every SEAM the app is supposed to construct, ⊥ 1 category of them.
V206: a number ∈ the SPEC is a claim like any other (V186). this spec asserted a display colour decoded to ~0.046; measurement says 0.0376. the wrong number had been copied into a source comment & read as researched for weeks. cite measurements, ⊥ recollections — & when correcting one, grep for its copies.
V204: a CANVAS RETAINS its last presented frame ∴ "the previews look rendered" is ⊥ evidence that anything is rendering NOW. I read retained pixels as a live picture & filed a critical bug on it. the live check is the FRAME COUNT, ⊥ the image.
V201: a composed test that STUBS the thing under suspicion proves the layers around it & says NOTHING about it. T267 drives a stubbed `loop` & ticks it by hand ∴ it proves edit→plan and is silent on whether `backend.loop` ever fires. ⊥ a defence of the code — a gap ∈ the test.
V200: a "we measured & stopped" decision is recorded W/ ITS NUMBERS & the script that produced them, ⊥ as "deferred". a bare deferral reads as laziness to the next person & gets re-litigated from scratch; the census is what makes the decision re-checkable when the assumption (graph size) changes.
V197: §V104 NARROWED — "if you WRITE it, you OWN it". an UNMODIFIED attribute passes downstream BY REFERENCE. V104's stated reason was an ID-DERIVATION CONVENTION (`pointPairId(nodeId, attr)`), ⊥ a physical hazard ∴ narrowing preserves the safety argument exactly. NUMBERS: a 4-node chain over a 7-attribute schema costs 56 buffers / 640 B per point / **480 B per point per frame of pure memcpy = 28.8 GB/s @ 1M points @60fps** — enough to saturate an integrated GPU before 1 useful instruction runs. copy-on-write: 20 buffers, 256 B/point, copy traffic ≈ 0. the HARD part is SWAP OWNERSHIP: consumers are found by WHO BINDS A `pairId`, ⊥ by reachability. that is its own task w/ its own test, ⊥ a footnote.
V198: point-node TRANSFORM COMPOSITION ORDER is PUBLISHED & pinned by test. Houdini publishes one; TD publishes none, & "which order" is the most expensive thing to get wrong & costs nothing to fix today.
V199b: compat mode is **⊥ a target** — decided, ⊥ deferred. baseline is Tier B CORE. on a compat adapter the capability report REFUSES point/geometry rendering LOUDLY (V12 style), ⊥ breaks silently. migration IS named & bounded: SoA pairs gain VERTEX usage & bind as instance-step vertex buffers — same buffers, same kernels, different binding point. a named migration w/o a GUARD is how a silent breakage ships ∴ the refusal is wired now.
V199: WebGPU **compat mode allows 0 storage buffers ∈ the VERTEX stage** (core allows 8). our render approach is vertex-pulling from SoA storage ∴ compat is a REWRITE, ⊥ a tuning problem. decide EXPLICITLY whether compat is a target before more of the point system assumes vertex-stage storage.
V195: V107's "∀ parameter takes ∀ mode" is leaf-only for the SCALAR-RETURNING kinds. AMENDED (T286): the constraint follows the KIND's return type, ⊥ the parameter's shape.
 • `expression`, `bind`, `driven` RETURN A NUMBER ∴ leaf-only. a container is static as a whole & its LEAVES (`stops[2].position`, `color.r`) are the moded things. that rationale is unchanged.
 • `map` returns a TYPED per-point value ∴ it ? address a COMPOUND HEAD under type-match (vec4f→color, vec3f→vector3), & scalar leaves keep working via `channel`. V113 already makes a slot @ the bare compound key legal, so the grammar needs nothing new.
 the alternative — a per-point colour as 4 slots naming 4 channels — is technically sufficient & HOSTILE to the person doing the obvious thing.
V196: a container parameter carrying COLOUR declares its space like `color` does, & the resolver decodes PER ENTRY. decoding at the container level (or ⊥ @ all) reproduces B8 — inspector shows 1 colour, GPU renders another — & a list makes it N times harder to notice.
V194: node TYPE STRINGS are 1 flat namespace across families ∴ a value-graph node that shares a concept w/ an image node is prefixed: `valueMath`, `valueLimit`, `valueFilter`. TD disambiguates by family suffix (TOP|CHOP) & we have ⊥ that. the collisions are ⊥ hypothetical — Limit, Math, Noise, Constant, Transform ∀ exist | will exist ∈ both families. the EXPORTED CONST ! match its type string ∴ `valueLimitNode`, ⊥ `limitNode` — a const named for the concept & typed for the family is how 2 modules silently claim 1 name.
V193: ∀ agent tool is REACHABLE ∈ the composed app — a composed-level test enumerates the surface & asserts each tool's port is live, ⊥ per-tool spot checks. "built, tested, ⊥ wired" has now happened 3× (B12 agent surface, T264 media, B23 render_preview) & each time every unit test was green. the enumeration is the only guard that scales: a NEW tool is covered the day it exists, ⊥ the day someone remembers.
V189: auto-layout is DETERMINISTIC — same graph → same positions, ∀ time. an agent that re-lays-out & gets different coordinates ⊥ reason about its own canvas, & a human gets churn ∈ every diff. ⊥ randomness, ⊥ iteration count, ⊥ insertion-order dependence.
V190: layout is a POSITION edit ∴ `nodePosition` classification — editor-only, ⊥ recompile, ⊥ resource rebuild (V1). laying out a 200-node graph ! cost 0 GPU work.
V191: ONE layout implementation, reached by both the keymap (`L`) & the bus command the agent calls (V78). a "layout for agents" that differs from the button is 2 products.
V192: MCP|WebMCP adapters are TRANSPORT + SCHEMA ONLY (V39). they live OUTSIDE `src/agent/**` ∴ the surface stays transport-free & headlessly testable — a server drags node|network deps, WebMCP drags DOM, & neither belongs ∈ the thing they both wrap.
V186b: retracting a wrong citation means finding EVERY copy of it. the invented "Premultiply TOP" claim lived ∈ 2 files; the 1st correction fixed 1 & left the other reading as researched fact for another 3 hours. `grep` the claim, ⊥ the file you happened to open.
V186: a comment citing an EXTERNAL fact is a claim. if nobody checked it, ⊥ write it — the next person treats it as researched. 2 invented TD citations shipped ∈ this catalogue before a survey caught them.
V187: `index.test.ts`'s type list catches a node registered w/ the wrong name. it ⊥ catch WRITTEN BUT NEVER REGISTERED — a shader + definition can sit unreferenced & every gate stays green. the catalogue-chain sweep is what must own that claim.
V188: POP survey vs §V104 — TD passes UNMODIFIED attributes downstream BY REFERENCE (copy-on-write); V104 mandates fresh pairs for ∀ a node outputs. V104's REASON is sound (aliased sim state across nodes). its COST is ~80 buffers where TD allocates 12. re-read before P3a allocates — ⊥ inherit the number by accident.
V183: know WHICH crossing costs. SCALARS crossing CPU↔GPU are ~free — a value graph is a handful of f32 & the uniform write already happens ∀ frame (V5's path). TEXTURES crossing are ⊥ free. ∴ the value graph is CPU-side BECAUSE it is scalars; the rule ⊥ generalize to pixels.
V184: ⊥ NEW GPU→CPU route. the ONLY readback is the existing ASYNC between-frames one (V48/V7/V144), 1 frame late by contract. ⊥ `mapAsync`-await inside the frame loop, ⊥ a "just this once" sync read. a stall is invisible ∈ a test & fatal ∈ a 60Hz loop.
V185: readbacks are COUNTED & SHOWN. N Analyze nodes = N readbacks/frame; the perf panel reports the count & total bytes ∴ a user who drops 20 of them SEES why it got slow, ⊥ guesses. an invisible cost gets paid repeatedly.
V179: value edges form a SEPARATE graph — CPU-side, topologically ordered, evaluated ∀ frame BEFORE the GPU plan. value nodes ⊥ enter the GPU plan & ⊥ allocate GPU resources. a value edge ⊥ a texture edge & the 2 ⊥ connect (V19 port typing already refuses it).
V180: a value node publishes NAMED CHANNELS, ⊥ 1 anonymous number. address = `node` (first channel) | `node:channel` (`mouse1:x`). single-channel is the degenerate case — Mouse has x,y & shipping only 1 forces 2 nodes for 1 device.
V181: a STATEFUL value node (Lag, Hold, Count, Slope) is unskippable (V155) & ! declare its reset (T214/T216). its state ⊥ a function of frame index ∴ a seek that ⊥ replay is ⊥ scrub-accurate (V170) & ! say so.
V182: Mouse publishes the SAME pointer the shaders read (`FrameEvaluationInput.pointer`, the shared block's `pointer`). ⊥ a 2nd DOM listener — 2 sources for 1 device drift by a frame & the CPU & GPU halves of one graph disagree about where the cursor is.
V177: project settings are DOCUMENT state — they serialize w/ the graph, mutate ONLY via `project.setSettings` (V29), bump the revision & make ONE undo entry ("Set frame rate", TD-style). `AppRuntime.settings` is a live VIEW of the store, ⊥ a snapshot.
V178: settings edits classify PER FIELD. `outputResolution`, `workingFormat`, `limits` = STRUCTURAL → recompile. `fps`, `previewFps`, `previewLongEdge` = ⊥ structural → ⊥ recompile, ⊥ resource rebuild. w/o this someone writes "any settings edit rebuilds the world" & dragging an fps field rebuilds every resource @ 60Hz.
V176: `time` ∈ expressions & the shared block is TIMELINE time = frameIndex/fps — uniform by construction. wall time is a SEPARATE name for when sync matters. a linear expression on `time` ! produce linear motion; if it visibly jitters the clock is wrong, ⊥ the user's expression.
V173b: LIVENESS has 3 sources, ⊥ 1: (a) a data EDGE to a sink, (b) a DRIVEN slot naming a value node's channel, (c) an `op()` REFERENCE ∈ an expression. ∀ consumer of liveness uses the SAME answer — `plan.pruned`, the example gate's dead-node check, cooking's dirty set, the UI's pruned badge. 4 places computing it separately = 4 chances to disagree, & they will.
V173: `pruned` means DEAD — "could have contributed GPU work & was excluded". a node that is NON-PLAN-RESIDENT BY DESIGN (the value trio: LFO, Constant, Timer — no ports, no passes) is ⊥ pruned, it was never a candidate. conflating them puts a `pruned` warning badge on a WORKING LFO & counts it ∈ "nodes pruned".
V172: a graph edit produces a rendered frame BY ITSELF — ⊥ waiting on an unrelated event (zoom, resize, pointer move, next param change) to nudge it. "renders when I zoom" means the render is riding someone else's invalidation & the edit path has no trigger of its own. measure FIRST-FRAME-AFTER-EDIT ∈ a composed test, ⊥ eyeball it.
V169: the timeline readout (frame, time, fps) reads the SAME `FrameEvaluationInput` the render consumed — ⊥ a parallel clock, ⊥ `performance.now`. a readout that can disagree w/ the picture is worse than no readout: it's the thing you check when you ⊥ trust the picture.
V170: SEEKING BACKWARD ∈ a graph w/ temporal state (feedback, Cache, point sim) ⊥ free — the state ∉ a function of frame index alone. ∴ a backward seek ! either RESET stateful nodes or SAY it ⊥ scrub-accurate. ⊥ silently show a state that belongs to a different history. forward seek = run the frames | same problem.
V171: resolution is a LADDER: project → component → node override (V-existing per-node). each level defaults to its parent & says WHERE its value came from ∈ the UI. an inherited value that looks authored is how someone edits the wrong level.
V167: a capability is ⊥ SHIPPED until a node can DECLARE it & a user can REACH it. T229 built `ExternalTextureResourceDescriptor` + `registerMediaSource` + upload gating, ∀ tested — & ⊥ node can request one ∴ `ScratchRequest` has no `external` kind. the plan layer & the backend layer are both green & the catalogue has no media node. same shape as B12.
V168: within 1 frame, PLAN ORDER = EXECUTION ORDER. vgpu `compute.dispatch()` submits IMMEDIATELY while render passes submit @ frame close ∴ ∀ dispatch ran before ∀ render pass regardless of plan order (B19). ⊥ observable ∈ any mock.
V165: `project.new` is a 4th DESTRUCTIVE verb (w/ open) — ! confirm when dirty, same rule as V93's open.
V166: an unsaved-work confirm offers SAVE as its primary action, ⊥ only discard|cancel. a 2-button "are you sure" forces cancel → save by hand → redo the action, & the user who wanted to keep their work is the one punished for it. 3 buttons: Save & continue | Discard | Cancel.
